1	---
2	name: Porting epic and workflow
3	description: SPI-238 epic for porting dev_sprint to dev — 13 tickets, 5-phase agent workflow, parallel lanes, dependency order
4	type: project
5	---
6	
7	Epic SPI-238 tracks porting all capability slices from dev_sprint to dev in dependency order.
8	
9	**Why:** dev_sprint has ~500 files of feature work (FS1-FS12, storage, validation, QC, LLM routing, API) that needs to land on dev as reviewable, tested PRs — not a raw merge.
10	
11	**How to apply:**
12	- 13 sub-tickets (SPI-225 through SPI-237), each one port slice
13	- SPI-225 (storage) and SPI-226 (prompt/LLM) already landed
14	- SPI-227 (document storage) in progress
15	- 4 parallel lanes possible; see epic description for dependency graph
16	- Every ticket follows a 5-phase workflow: Research → Harmonize → Ask questions (BLOCKING) → Implement → Open PR
17	- Phase 2 harmonization findings go as a comment on the Linear ticket for discussion
18	- Phase 3 is a hard gate — no implementation without explicit user approval
19	- PRs reference the SPI ticket number in the title
20	- Don't move to dependent tickets until blocking PRs are merged on dev
21	- No need to pin dev_sprint commit — use whatever is current at time of work
22	- Tickets with dedicated test plans (FS5, FS6, FS7) must follow them; others must propose test approach for approval
23	- Status vocabulary from AGENTS.md: pending, in_progress, implemented (code exists but not verified), verified (all gates passed)
24	
```

Now I have a complete picture. Here is the full report.

---

## Complete dev_sprint Document Storage Authority Analysis for SPI-227

### Source files on `origin/dev_sprint`

---

### 1. `packages/storage/document_store.py` -- NEW FILE (does not exist on `dev`)

This is the central new module. It is the **document byte authority** over artifacts and information-node locators. ~500 lines.

**Key constants:**
- `DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND = "document_blob"` -- the canonical `artifacts.kind` value
- `_DURABLE_REF_KEYS` -- frozenset of keys that count as stable regeneration references (`api_endpoint`, `artifact_id`, `db_ref`, `external_dataset_id`, `source_api`, `source_url`, `stable_external_ref`, `third_party_spec_id`)

**Dataclasses (frozen, slotted):**
- `StoredDocumentBlob` -- artifact_id, object_key, content_type, sha256, size_bytes, run_id, job_id, question_id, file_location dict, created_at
- `EligibleDocumentBlobRef` -- node_id, artifact_id, object_key, sha256, content_type, source_url, regeneration dict, file_location dict
- `RegisteredGraphDocumentBlob` -- node_id, artifact_id, object_key, sha256, content_type, size_bytes, source_url, file_location dict
- `GraphDocumentBlobRegistrationResult` -- registered list + skipped list, with `to_payload()` returning schema `fs2_document_blob_registration_v1`

**Core public functions:**

1. **`build_document_file_location(...)`** -- Constructs the canonical `information_file_location_v1` JSON shape:
   - `schema_version: "information_file_location_v1"`
   - `storage_kind: "artifact"`
   - `artifact_kind: "document_blob"`
   - Includes artifact_id, object_key, sha256, content_type, size_bytes, source_url, retrieval_source_id, retrieved_at, regeneration dict, fs11_concern_refs list
   - Validates regeneration requires either source_url or a durable ref key
   - Validates sha256 is exactly 64 hex chars

2. **`register_document_blob_artifact(...)`** -- Creates or updates an `ArtifactRow` with `kind="document_blob"`. Upsert logic: if artifact_id exists and kind is not `document_blob`, raises ValueError. Returns `StoredDocumentBlob`.

3. **`attach_document_blob_to_information_node(...)`** -- Wires an existing artifact to an existing `InformationNodeRow` by:
   - Setting `information_node.file_location` to the built file_location JSON
   - Setting `information_node.content_type` from the artifact
   - Optionally setting `information_node.source` from source_url
   - Also nests `file_location` into `information_node.detail["detail"]["file_location"]`

4. **`register_fs2_graph_document_blobs(graph, run_id, question_id, ...)`** -- The FS2 bridge function. Iterates graph nodes of type `evidence` or `information`:
   - Reads the file from disk via `detail.document_link` or `detail.local_path`
   - Computes SHA256 of the file bytes and guesses content_type from extension
   - Generates artifact_id as `doc_{sha256[:16]}_{sha1(run|question|node|key)[:16]}`
   - Builds regeneration dict (mode: source_url, source_api, or db_ref)
   - Calls `register_document_blob_artifact` + `attach_document_blob_to_information_node`
   - Writes file_location back to the in-memory graph node's data dict
   - Returns `GraphDocumentBlobRegistrationResult` with registered/skipped lists

5. **`list_eligible_document_blob_refs(run_id, question_id, ...)`** -- Eligibility query for downstream consumption. Uses a multi-join: `InformationNodeRow -> NodeRow -> EdgeRow -> ReferenceEdgeRow` where `reference_type = "evidence"`. Three gates:
   - FS11 QC gate decision must exist and be `gate_status = "pass"`
   - `file_location.artifact_kind` must be `"document_blob"` and `storage_kind` must be `"artifact"`
   - SHA256 in file_location must match the artifact row's sha256
   - Artifact's run_id and question_id must match

6. **`build_fs10_document_replay_payloads(run_id, question_id, ...)`** -- Produces `fs10_document_replay_ref_v1` payloads from eligible refs. Each payload includes upstream_fs="FS2", document_producing_step="STEP_2A-doc-or-STEP_4", and carries through all artifact metadata including regeneration and fs11_concern_refs.

**How `artifacts.kind='document_blob'` is used:**
- Set on `ArtifactRow.kind` during `register_document_blob_artifact`
- Checked by `attach_document_blob_to_information_node` (rejects non-document_blob artifacts)
- Checked by `list_eligible_document_blob_refs` (filters on `file_location.artifact_kind == DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND`)
- Included in replay payloads as `artifact_kind`

**How `information_nodes.file_location` is referenced:**
- Written by `attach_document_blob_to_information_node` with the full `information_file_location_v1` schema
- Read by `list_eligible_document_blob_refs` to check artifact_kind, storage_kind, sha256, and extract source_url/regeneration
- Also nested into `information_nodes.detail["detail"]["file_location"]` for backward compatibility

**SHA256 / byte count handling:**
- SHA256 validated as exactly 64-char hex string via `_clean_sha256`
- SHA256 stored on both `ArtifactRow.sha256` and inside `file_location.sha256`
- Eligibility check compares `file_location.sha256` against `ArtifactRow.sha256`
- Size stored on `ArtifactRow.size_bytes` and optionally in `file_location.size_bytes`
- In graph registration, computed from actual file bytes via `hashlib.sha256(data_bytes).hexdigest()`

**FS2 evidence / FS11 QC integration:**
- `register_fs2_graph_document_blobs` is the FS2 bridge (runs post-graph-persistence)
- `list_eligible_document_blob_refs` gates on `FS11QCGateDecisionRow.gate_status == "pass"`
- `build_document_file_location` accepts `fs11_concern_refs` list (validated: each must have run_id, question_id, concern_id)
- `build_fs10_document_replay_payloads` carries `fs11_concern_refs` through to FS10

---

### 2. `packages/storage/database.py` -- MODIFIED (523 changed lines between dev and dev_sprint)

The relevant model definitions for document storage:

**`ArtifactRow`** (line 734): `artifacts` table with columns: artifact_id (PK, String(64)), job_id (FK to jobs), run_id (FK to runs), question_id, kind (String(128)), label, content_type, object_key, size_bytes, sha256 (String(128)), created_at. The `kind` column is where `"document_blob"` is stored.

**`InformationNodeRow`** (line 577): `information_nodes` table with: node_id (PK, FK to nodes), file_location (JSON, not nullable, default=dict), source, content_type, publication_date, detail (JSON). The `file_location` column is the primary locator that document_store.py writes to.

**`NodeRow`** (line 470): `nodes` table with denormalized question_id and run_id used by the eligibility join.

**`EdgeRow`** (line 593) + **`ReferenceEdgeRow`** (line 654): Used by eligibility query to find evidence edges.

**`FS11QCGateDecisionRow`** (line 2388): `fs11_qc_gate_decisions` with composite PK (run_id, question_id), gate_status, review_state, decision_source, summary_json, etc. This is the gate that `list_eligible_document_blob_refs` checks.

Note: There is no separate `document_blobs` table -- document storage is entirely done through the existing `artifacts` table (kind="document_blob") + `information_nodes.file_location`.

---

### 3. `packages/storage/sql_repositories.py` -- MODIFIED (90 changed lines)

**Document-storage-related changes between dev and dev_sprint:**

The file_location handling in `_save_node_type_detail` (for `NodeType.INFORMATION`) is identical on both branches -- it reads `file_location` from `data.detail.file_location` or `data.file_location`, persists to `InformationNodeRow.file_location`, and extracts content_type.

The `_load_node_type_detail` function nests `file_location` back into `detail.detail.file_location` when loading.

**Key differences (not directly document_store.py related but in the same PR scope):**
- Added `import hashlib` and `ArtifactRow` import
- Extracted `_node_type_for_record()` function (now raises ValueError for type="question" instead of silently mapping to DRIVER)
- Removed `"question": NodeType.DRIVER` from `_NODE_TYPE_MAP`
- Added `edge_effect_artifact_id` handling to `_save_edge_type_detail` / `_load_edge_type_detail` (new FS5 edge effect artifact support via `_ensure_edge_effect_artifact`)
- Changed `import json as _json` to use top-level `json` import

---

### 4. `packages/domain/qc/research_defensibility_store.py` -- MODIFIED (376 diff lines)

**New additions for document storage authority:**

- **`StoredRuntimeResearchDefensibilityEvidence`** dataclass: score, gate_status, review_state, decision_source, validation_run_created
- **`_is_hard_fail_gate_score()`**: checks hard_fail or rating=="Fail"
- **`_ensure_runtime_validation_run()`**: Creates/updates FS10ValidationRunRow for runtime FS2 evidence with set_name "fs2_runtime_authority:P-FS2-01"
- **`_derive_runtime_axes()`**: Produces conservative process scores from DB-backed document refs (not the production analytical scorer)
- **`_runtime_gate_summary()`**: Builds gate summary JSON (schema `fs2_runtime_research_defensibility_gate_v1`)
- **`_upsert_runtime_gate_decision()`**: Creates/updates `FS11QCGateDecisionRow` with automatic gate_status based on score
- **`persist_fs2_runtime_research_defensibility_evidence()`**: Main public function that orchestrates runtime evidence flow: ensures validation run, persists score, upserts gate decision

**The FS11 QC integration is critical**: `list_eligible_document_blob_refs` in document_store.py queries `FS11QCGateDecisionRow`, and this module is what creates those gate decision rows at runtime. The `persist_fs2_runtime_research_defensibility_evidence` function is the runtime bridge that makes document blobs eligible for downstream replay by ensuring the QC gate passes.

**Also expanded**: `persist_fs2_research_defensibility_score` now writes many more scorecard columns (scorecard_id, producer_fs, upstream_fs, subject_type, score_family, axis_ref, surface_refs_json, etc.) and changed `"families": ["research_defensibility"]` to `"families": ["Research"]`.

**Key architectural invariants:**
- No `document_blobs` table -- documents live in `artifacts` (kind="document_blob") + `information_nodes.file_location`
- Eligibility is triple-gated: evidence edge exists, FS11 QC gate passes, SHA256 matches
- file_location uses schema `information_file_location_v1` with `storage_kind="artifact"` and `artifact_kind="document_blob"`
- FS10 replay payloads use schema `fs10_document_replay_ref_v1`
- Regeneration requires either a source_url or a durable reference key

The gate specifications (P-DB-01 through P-DB-06) don't exist yet as documents. Based on the information in the Linear issue, these are referenced but not yet fully documented. Let me compile the comprehensive summary based on what we have:

## Comprehensive Summary: SPI-227 Document Storage Authority Design Docs

Based on my review of the available design documentation, here's what the files specify about document storage for the SPI-227 porting ticket:

### 1. **Document Storage Design** (`docs/plans/shared_architecture/document-storage-design.md`)

**How Document Blobs Should Be Stored:**
- Document bytes use `artifacts.kind='document_blob'` as the primary storage mechanism
- No new dedicated `document_blobs` table is introduced
- The existing `artifacts` table is used with these required columns:
  - `kind` = literal `'document_blob'`
  - `object_key` - local artifact key or object-store key for the bytes
  - `content_type` - MIME type (e.g., `application/pdf`, `text/html`)
  - `sha256` - hex digest of bytes (critical for integrity)
  - `size_bytes` - byte count for budgets and sanity checks
  - `run_id` - producing run
  - `job_id` (optional) - batch context when available
  - `question_id` (required for question-scoped research)

**File Location Authority:**
- `information_nodes.file_location` is the canonical locator for documents and remains as a `JSON NOT NULL` column
- For artifact-backed documents, the file_location must use this standardized shape:
  ```json
  {
    "schema_version": "information_file_location_v1",
    "storage_kind": "artifact",
    "artifact_id": "art_...",
    "artifact_kind": "document_blob",
    "object_key": "runs/.../document.pdf",
    "sha256": "<hex>",
    "content_type": "application/pdf",
    "size_bytes": 12345,
    "source_url": "https://example.com/report.pdf",
    "retrieval_source_id": "src_...",
    "retrieved_at": "2026-05-06T00:00:00Z",
    "regeneration": {
      "mode": "source_url",
      "idempotency_key": "provider/source/document"
    },
    "fs11_concern_refs": [
      {
        "run_id": "run_...",
        "question_id": "q_...",
        "concern_id": "concern_..."
      }
    ]
  }
  ```

**SHA256 Hashing and Byte Count Requirements:**
- SHA256 must be computed over the exact bytes consumed by downstream readers
- Byte count (`size_bytes`) is required for budgets and sanity checks
- Artifact row serves as the DB-backed byte handle
- SHA256 matching between `artifacts.sha256` and `file_location.sha256` is validation critical

**FS11-Gated Evidence Eligibility Surface:**
- There is NO document-local gate enum in this design
- Automatic FS3 consumption requires ALL of:
  1. An `information_nodes` row with `file_location.artifact_kind='document_blob'`
  2. Linked `artifacts` row with `kind='document_blob'` and `sha256` matching `file_location.sha256`
  3. A `reference_edges` row with `reference_type='evidence'` or `reference_type='source'`
  4. An FS11 gate decision for `(run_id, question_id)` with `fs11_qc_gate_decisions.gate_status='pass'`
- Non-pass, review-required, forced-continue, or unresolved FS11 decisions keep material diagnostic-only
- Hard-fails from FS11 can block downstream consumption

**FS10 Frozen Replay Requirements:**
- When FS10 mirrors document evidence into `fs10_validation_component_artifacts.payload_json`, must include:
  - `schema_version`, `run_id`, `question_id`, `upstream_fs`, producing step
  - `artifact_id`, `artifact_kind`, `object_key`, `sha256`, `content_type`, `size_bytes`
  - `information_node_id` and driver/indicator refs through `reference_edges`
  - `source_url`, `retrieval_source_id`, `retrieved_at`, `regeneration`
  - FS11 scorecard ids, gate decision summary, and concern refs as `(run_id, question_id, concern_id)` objects
- Replay consumers must verify stored `sha256` before using bytes
- Missing local files cannot trigger historical mutation; rehydration only through documented `regeneration` path

**Critical Process Flows:**
- **Ingest:** FS2 retrieval → compute sha256 → write to artifact repository → register `artifacts` row → write `information_nodes.file_location` → link `reference_edges` → FS11 gate decision
- **Read:** FS3 queries `information_nodes` + `reference_edges` → checks FS11 gate decision → validates sha256 matches → reads `artifacts.object_key` → consumes bytes
- **FS11 Review:** FS11 creates `fs11_qc_scorecards`, `fs11_qc_concerns`, `fs11_qc_gate_decisions` that govern downstream consumption

### 2. **FS2 Integration Design** (`docs/plans/FS2/fs2-fs10-fs11-integration-design.md`)

**FS2 Evidence Integration Paths:**
- FS2 is the canonical producer of document artifacts (STEP_2A-doc and STEP_4)
- FS2 emits:
  - `sources.json` - document references and metadata
  - `driver_indicators.json` - extracted entities
  - `historic_series.json` - historical data artifacts
  - `event_history.json` - event sequences
  - Canonical FS10 component artifact: `runtime/fs10_validation/component_artifacts/<run>/<question_id>/FS2/retrieval.json`
  - FS11 QC payload in `qc/index.json`

**FS2 Scorecard Axes for Document Quality (Initial):**
- routing_fit
- source_diversity
- evidence_coverage
- document_quality
- historical_branch_adequacy
- provenance_quality
- local_doc_consistency
- ai_mode_exposure

**FS2-Specific Concerns:**
- Document artifacts must carry evidence of provenance (source_url, retrieval_source_id)
- AI-mode usage flags must be explicit for reproducibility
- Local-doc handling must be traceable
- Retrieval validation moved from stage-local to FS10 component validation

### 3. **FS10 Validation Design** (limited scope for document validation)

**Component Validation for Documents:**
- FS2 `retrieval.json` artifact marked as `component_validation` by default
- FS10 validates with diagnostic checks first, then with reviewed labels where available
- Component status flags: `ok`, `failed`, or `unsupported`
- FS10 does NOT use document artifacts in role-training (Section 3 boundary preserved)
- Hard failures in FS2 component validation propagate to block downstream consumption

**Per-FS Component Validation Criteria:**
- FS2 artifacts must show "sufficient evidence or complete diagnostic summary"
- Failure marked if "retrieval errored hard or diagnostics missing"
- Unsupported if "no relevant sources for question domain"

### 4. **FS11 QC Integration**

**FS11's Role in Document Gating:**
- FS11 creates `fs11_qc_gate_decisions` with `gate_status='pass'|'review_required'|'forced_continue'|'unresolved'`
- FS11 creates `fs11_qc_concerns` rows referenced by `(run_id, question_id, concern_id)` tuples
- Hard-fail concerns block automatic FS3 consumption
- Advisory concerns keep material available for diagnostic review but flag quality issues

**Cross-FS Consumer Matrix:**
- **FS2** - producer: registers artifacts, writes file_location, links reference_edges, records FS11 refs
- **FS3** - reader/transformer: reads only artifact-backed evidence whose FS11 gate passes; may emit transformed artifacts through same discriminator
- **FS10** - mirror/replay: freezes stable document refs and sha256 evidence in validation payloads
- **FS11** - reviewer: writes scorecards, concerns, gate decisions that govern consumption
- **FS7** - audit reader: follows information nodes and reference edges; byte reads optional
- **FS8/FS9** - resolution readers: follow graph evidence refs and FS11 decisions; don't treat object keys as authority

### 5. **Design Discipline Notes**

**What's NOT Allowed:**
- No JSON files as system-of-record for documents (DB-backed only)
- No new `document_blobs` table
- No document-local status enum (FS11 gate decisions are authoritative)
- No new object-store infrastructure beyond existing artifact repository contract
- No new product-data persistence outside DB-backed authorities

**Critical Implementation Constraints:**
- Bytes behind `object_key` are artifact bytes; they may live on local storage in dev and object storage in production, but product decisions resolve through DB rows and replay payloads
- `source_url` may be null only when `regeneration` names a DB-backed, idempotent reconstruction path
- `regeneration.idempotency_key` must be stable across runs to support replay
- Test coverage must include: artifact registration, information-node locator round-trip, FS11-derived read gating, sha mismatch rejection, FS10 replay payload completeness

### 6. **Test Requirements (Gates P-DB-01 to P-DB-06, P-FS2-01)**

**Note:** The specific gate definitions (P-DB-01 through P-DB-06 and P-FS2-01) referenced in the Linear issue do not yet exist as documented files. However, based on the design docs, these gates are expected to cover:

**Inferred P-DB Gates (Document/Artifact Storage):**
- P-DB-01: Document blob registration with `kind='document_blob'`
- P-DB-02: SHA256 hashing and byte count integrity
- P-DB-03: `information_nodes.file_location` locator authority with standardized shape
- P-DB-04: Reference edges linking documents to driver/indicator nodes
- P-DB-05: FS11 gate decision enforcement on consumption
- P-DB-06: FS10 frozen replay payload completeness with document metadata

**P-FS2-01 (FS2 Integration):**
- FS2 evidence integration path coverage: retrieval artifacts, source tracking, local-doc handling

### 7. **Owned Files (No Other Ticket Should Touch)**
- `packages/storage/document_store.py`
- `tests/test_document_storage_authority.py`
- `tests/test_artifact_repository_contract.py`

### 8. **Shared Files (Coordinate with Other Tickets)**
- `packages/storage/database.py` — shared; only touch document/artifact columns
- `packages/storage/sql_repositories.py` — shared; only touch document/artifact repository methods

### 9. **Acceptance Criteria Summary**
- Document artifacts register with `kind='document_blob'`
- SHA256 hashing and byte count tracked for all documents
- `information_nodes.file_location` used as locator authority
- No JSON files as system-of-record for documents (DB-backed only)
- No new `document_blobs` table introduced
- All standard repo gates pass (ruff check, ruff format, pytest suite)
- All document-storage-specific test suites pass

The design establishes a clean separation of concerns where `artifacts` table owns byte metadata and location, `information_nodes.file_location` owns the standardized locator shape, `reference_edges` connects documents to the graph, and `fs11_qc_gate_decisions` provides the eligibility gate for downstream consumption.
```

"""Document byte authority over artifacts and information-node locators."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import mimetypes
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select

from packages.storage.database import (
    ArtifactRow,
    EdgeRow,
    FS11QCGateDecisionRow,
    InformationNodeRow,
    NodeRow,
    ReferenceEdgeRow,
    ensure_database_schema,
    utc_now,
)
from packages.storage.runtime_config import StorageRuntimeConfig

DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND = "document_blob"
_DURABLE_REF_KEYS = frozenset(
    {
        "api_endpoint",
        "artifact_id",
        "db_ref",
        "external_dataset_id",
        "source_api",
        "source_url",
        "stable_external_ref",
        "third_party_spec_id",
    }
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StoredDocumentBlob:
    artifact_id: str
    object_key: str
    content_type: str
    sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    run_id: str | None
    job_id: str | None
    question_id: str | None
    file_location: dict[str, Any]
    created_at: datetime


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EligibleDocumentBlobRef:
    node_id: str
    artifact_id: str
    object_key: str
    sha256: str
    content_type: str
    source_url: str | None
    regeneration: dict[str, Any]
    file_location: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RegisteredGraphDocumentBlob:
    node_id: str
    artifact_id: str
    object_key: str
    sha256: str
    content_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    source_url: str | None
    file_location: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GraphDocumentBlobRegistrationResult:
    registered: list[RegisteredGraphDocumentBlob]
    skipped: list[dict[str, str]]

    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "schema_version": "fs2_document_blob_registration_v1",
            "registered_count": len(self.registered),
            "skipped_count": len(self.skipped),
            "registered": [
                {
                    "node_id": item.node_id,
                    "artifact_id": item.artifact_id,
                    "object_key": item.object_key,
                    "sha256": item.sha256,
                    "content_type": item.content_type,
                    "size_bytes": item.size_bytes,
                    "source_url": item.source_url,
                }
                for item in self.registered
            ],
            "skipped": [dict(item) for item in self.skipped],
        }


def _clean_required(value: str | None, *, field_name: str) -> str:
    cleaned = str(value or "").strip()
    if not cleaned:
        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} is required")
    return cleaned


def _clean_optional(value: str | None) -> str | None:
    cleaned = str(value or "").strip()
    return cleaned or None


def _clean_sha256(value: str | None) -> str:
    cleaned = _clean_required(value, field_name="sha256")
    if len(cleaned) != 64:
        raise ValueError("sha256 must be a 64-character hex digest")
    return cleaned


def _has_durable_ref(*, source_url: str | None, regeneration: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    if _clean_optional(source_url):
        return True
    return any(bool(regeneration.get(key)) for key in _DURABLE_REF_KEYS)


def _validate_regeneration(*, source_url: str | None, regeneration: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if _has_durable_ref(source_url=source_url, regeneration=regeneration):
        return
    raise ValueError("document blobs require source_url or a stable DB/API/artifact regeneration reference")


def _validate_fs11_refs(refs: list[dict[str, Any]] | None) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    normalized: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    for item in list(refs or []):
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise ValueError("fs11_concern_refs must be JSON objects")
        normalized.append(
            {
                "run_id": _clean_required(str(item.get("run_id") or ""), field_name="fs11_concern_refs.run_id"),
                "question_id": _clean_required(str(item.get("question_id") or ""), field_name="fs11_concern_refs.question_id"),
                "concern_id": _clean_required(str(item.get("concern_id") or ""), field_name="fs11_concern_refs.concern_id"),
            }
        )
    return normalized


def build_document_file_location(
    *,
    artifact_id: str,
    object_key: str,
    sha256: str,
    content_type: str | None = None,
    size_bytes: int | None = None,
    source_url: str | None = None,
    retrieval_source_id: str | None = None,
    retrieved_at: str | None = None,
    regeneration: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    fs11_concern_refs: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    regeneration_json = dict(regeneration or {})
    _validate_regeneration(source_url=source_url, regeneration=regeneration_json)
    return {
        "schema_version": "information_file_location_v1",
        "storage_kind": "artifact",
        "artifact_id": _clean_required(artifact_id, field_name="artifact_id"),
        "artifact_kind": DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND,
        "object_key": _clean_required(object_key, field_name="object_key"),
        "sha256": _clean_sha256(sha256),
        "content_type": _clean_optional(content_type),
        "size_bytes": int(size_bytes) if size_bytes is not None else None,
        "source_url": _clean_optional(source_url),
        "retrieval_source_id": _clean_optional(retrieval_source_id),
        "retrieved_at": _clean_optional(retrieved_at),
        "regeneration": regeneration_json,
        "fs11_concern_refs": _validate_fs11_refs(fs11_concern_refs),
    }


def _artifact_to_document_blob(row: ArtifactRow, *, file_location: dict[str, Any]) -> StoredDocumentBlob:
    return StoredDocumentBlob(
        artifact_id=str(row.artifact_id),
        object_key=str(row.object_key),
        content_type=str(row.content_type),
        sha256=str(row.sha256),
        size_bytes=int(row.size_bytes),
        run_id=str(row.run_id) if row.run_id is not None else None,
        job_id=str(row.job_id) if row.job_id is not None else None,
        question_id=str(row.question_id) if row.question_id is not None else None,
        file_location=dict(file_location),
        created_at=row.created_at,
    )


def register_document_blob_artifact(
    *,
    artifact_id: str,
    object_key: str,
    content_type: str,
    sha256: str,
    size_bytes: int,
    label: str | None = None,
    run_id: str | None = None,
    job_id: str | None = None,
    question_id: str | None = None,
    source_url: str | None = None,
    retrieval_source_id: str | None = None,
    retrieved_at: str | None = None,
    regeneration: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    fs11_concern_refs: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
) -> StoredDocumentBlob:
    file_location = build_document_file_location(
        artifact_id=artifact_id,
        object_key=object_key,
        sha256=sha256,
        content_type=content_type,
        size_bytes=size_bytes,
        source_url=source_url,
        retrieval_source_id=retrieval_source_id,
        retrieved_at=retrieved_at,
        regeneration=regeneration,
        fs11_concern_refs=fs11_concern_refs,
    )
    database = ensure_database_schema(storage_config)
    normalized_artifact_id = str(file_location["artifact_id"])
    with database.session_scope() as session:
        row = session.get(ArtifactRow, normalized_artifact_id)
        if row is None:
            row = ArtifactRow(
                artifact_id=normalized_artifact_id,
                job_id=_clean_optional(job_id),
                run_id=_clean_optional(run_id),
                question_id=_clean_optional(question_id),
                kind=DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND,
                label=str(label or "document blob").strip() or "document blob",
                content_type=_clean_required(content_type, field_name="content_type"),
                object_key=str(file_location["object_key"]),
                size_bytes=int(size_bytes),
                sha256=str(file_location["sha256"]),
                created_at=utc_now(),
            )
            session.add(row)
        else:
            if str(row.kind) != DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND:
                raise ValueError(f"artifact_id {normalized_artifact_id!r} is not a document_blob artifact")
            row.job_id = _clean_optional(job_id)
            row.run_id = _clean_optional(run_id)
            row.question_id = _clean_optional(question_id)
            row.label = str(label or row.label or "document blob").strip() or "document blob"
            row.content_type = _clean_required(content_type, field_name="content_type")
            row.object_key = str(file_location["object_key"])
            row.size_bytes = int(size_bytes)
            row.sha256 = str(file_location["sha256"])
        session.flush()
        stored = _artifact_to_document_blob(row, file_location=file_location)
    return stored


def attach_document_blob_to_information_node(
    *,
    node_id: str,
    artifact_id: str,
    source_url: str | None = None,
    retrieval_source_id: str | None = None,
    retrieved_at: str | None = None,
    regeneration: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    fs11_concern_refs: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    database = ensure_database_schema(storage_config)
    normalized_node_id = _clean_required(node_id, field_name="node_id")
    normalized_artifact_id = _clean_required(artifact_id, field_name="artifact_id")
    with database.session_scope() as session:
        artifact = session.get(ArtifactRow, normalized_artifact_id)
        if artifact is None:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown artifact_id: {normalized_artifact_id!r}")
        if str(artifact.kind) != DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND:
            raise ValueError(f"artifact_id {normalized_artifact_id!r} is not a document_blob artifact")
        information_node = session.get(InformationNodeRow, normalized_node_id)
        if information_node is None:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown information node_id: {normalized_node_id!r}")
        file_location = build_document_file_location(
            artifact_id=normalized_artifact_id,
            object_key=str(artifact.object_key),
            sha256=str(artifact.sha256),
            content_type=str(artifact.content_type),
            size_bytes=int(artifact.size_bytes),
            source_url=source_url,
            retrieval_source_id=retrieval_source_id,
            retrieved_at=retrieved_at,
            regeneration=regeneration,
            fs11_concern_refs=fs11_concern_refs,
        )
        information_node.file_location = file_location
        information_node.content_type = str(artifact.content_type)
        if source_url:
            information_node.source = str(source_url).strip()
        detail = dict(information_node.detail or {})
        nested_detail = dict(detail.get("detail") or {})
        nested_detail["file_location"] = file_location
        detail["detail"] = nested_detail
        information_node.detail = detail
    return file_location


def _node_value(node: Any, key: str) -> str:
    return str(getattr(node, key, "") or "").strip()


def _node_data(node: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
    return dict(data) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}


def _nested_payload(data: dict[str, Any], key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    value = data.get(key)
    return dict(value) if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def _node_detail(data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    detail = _nested_payload(data, "detail")
    if detail:
        return detail
    return {
        str(key): value
        for key, value in data.items()
        if key not in {"question_id", "question_source", "source_tag", "origin", "provenance"}
    }


def _node_provenance(data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _nested_payload(data, "provenance")


def _first_text(*values: Any) -> str | None:
    for value in values:
        text = str(value or "").strip()
        if text:
            return text
    return None


def _source_payloads(provenance: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [dict(item) for item in (provenance.get("sources") or []) if isinstance(item, dict)]


def _source_url_for_document(*, detail: dict[str, Any], sources: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str | None:
    candidates: list[Any] = [
        detail.get("source_url"),
        detail.get("pdf_url"),
        detail.get("url"),
    ]
    for source in sources:
        candidates.extend([source.get("source_url"), source.get("pdf_url"), source.get("url")])
    text = _first_text(*candidates)
    if text and text.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
        return text
    return None


def _retrieval_source_id(*, detail: dict[str, Any], sources: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str | None:
    source = sources[0] if sources else {}
    provider = _first_text(source.get("provider"), detail.get("provider")) or "unknown"
    source_ref = _first_text(
        source.get("entry_id"),
        source.get("source_id"),
        source.get("document_number"),
        source.get("source_path"),
        detail.get("document_key"),
        detail.get("filename"),
    )
    if not source_ref:
        return provider
    return f"{provider}:{source_ref}"


def _retrieved_at(*, detail: dict[str, Any], sources: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    source = sources[0] if sources else {}
    return (
        _first_text(
            detail.get("retrieved_at"),
            detail.get("downloaded_at"),
            source.get("retrieved_at"),
            source.get("downloaded_at"),
        )
        or utc_now().isoformat()
    )


def _artifact_id_for_document(*, run_id: str, question_id: str, node_id: str, object_key: str, sha256: str) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha1(f"{run_id}|{question_id}|{node_id}|{object_key}".encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    return f"doc_{sha256[:16]}_{digest}"


def _regeneration_for_document(
    *,
    artifact_id: str,
    node_id: str,
    detail: dict[str, Any],
    sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
    source_url: str | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if source_url:
        return {
            "mode": "source_url",
            "source_url": source_url,
            "idempotency_key": source_url,
        }
    source = sources[0] if sources else {}
    provider = _first_text(source.get("provider"), detail.get("provider"))
    stable_ref = _first_text(source.get("entry_id"), source.get("source_id"), source.get("document_number"))
    if provider and stable_ref:
        return {
            "mode": "source_api",
            "source_api": provider,
            "stable_external_ref": stable_ref,
            "artifact_id": artifact_id,
        }
    return {
        "mode": "db_ref",
        "db_ref": f"information_nodes:{node_id}",
        "artifact_id": artifact_id,
        "document_key": _clean_optional(str(detail.get("document_key") or "")),
    }


def _write_file_location_back_to_graph_node(node: Any, file_location: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    data = _node_data(node)
    detail = _node_detail(data)
    detail["file_location"] = dict(file_location)
    data["detail"] = detail
    try:
        node.data = data
    except Exception:
        return


def register_fs2_graph_document_blobs(
    *,
    graph: Any,
    run_id: str,
    question_id: str,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
    strict: bool = False,
) -> GraphDocumentBlobRegistrationResult:
    """Register FS2 evidence-node document bytes as artifact-backed locators.

    This bridge is intentionally idempotent. It runs after graph persistence so
    `information_nodes` already exist, then updates their `file_location` JSON
    with the artifact-backed shape that FS3 and FS10 can replay.
    """

    normalized_run_id = _clean_required(run_id, field_name="run_id")
    normalized_question_id = _clean_required(question_id, field_name="question_id")
    registered: list[RegisteredGraphDocumentBlob] = []
    skipped: list[dict[str, str]] = []

    for node in (getattr(graph, "nodes", {}) or {}).values():
        node_type = _node_value(node, "type").lower()
        if node_type not in {"evidence", "information"}:
            continue
        node_id = _node_value(node, "id") or _node_value(node, "node_id")
        if not node_id:
            continue
        data = _node_data(node)
        if str(data.get("question_id") or "").strip() not in {"", normalized_question_id}:
            continue
        detail = _node_detail(data)
        provenance = _node_provenance(data)
        sources = _source_payloads(provenance)
        object_key = _first_text(detail.get("document_link"), detail.get("local_path"), *(source.get("local_path") for source in sources))
        if not object_key:
            skipped.append({"node_id": node_id, "reason": "missing_document_link"})
            continue
        path = Path(object_key)
        if not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
            skipped.append({"node_id": node_id, "reason": "missing_document_bytes", "object_key": object_key})
            continue
        data_bytes = path.read_bytes()
        sha256 = hashlib.sha256(data_bytes).hexdigest()
        content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or "application/octet-stream"
        source_url = _source_url_for_document(detail=detail, sources=sources)
        artifact_id = _artifact_id_for_document(
            run_id=normalized_run_id,
            question_id=normalized_question_id,
            node_id=node_id,
            object_key=object_key,
            sha256=sha256,
        )
        regeneration = _regeneration_for_document(
            artifact_id=artifact_id,
            node_id=node_id,
            detail=detail,
            sources=sources,
            source_url=source_url,
        )
        retrieval_source_id = _retrieval_source_id(detail=detail, sources=sources)
        retrieved_at = _retrieved_at(detail=detail, sources=sources)
        try:
            stored = register_document_blob_artifact(
                artifact_id=artifact_id,
                object_key=object_key,
                content_type=content_type,
                sha256=sha256,
                size_bytes=len(data_bytes),
                label=str(getattr(node, "label", "") or path.name).strip() or path.name,
                run_id=normalized_run_id,
                question_id=normalized_question_id,
                source_url=source_url,
                retrieval_source_id=retrieval_source_id,
                retrieved_at=retrieved_at,
                regeneration=regeneration,
                storage_config=storage_config,
            )
            file_location = attach_document_blob_to_information_node(
                node_id=node_id,
                artifact_id=stored.artifact_id,
                source_url=source_url,
                retrieval_source_id=retrieval_source_id,
                retrieved_at=retrieved_at,
                regeneration=regeneration,
                storage_config=storage_config,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            if strict:
                raise
            skipped.append({"node_id": node_id, "reason": "registration_rejected", "message": str(exc)})
            continue
        _write_file_location_back_to_graph_node(node, file_location)
        registered.append(
            RegisteredGraphDocumentBlob(
                node_id=node_id,
                artifact_id=stored.artifact_id,
                object_key=stored.object_key,
                sha256=stored.sha256,
                content_type=stored.content_type,
                size_bytes=stored.size_bytes,
                source_url=source_url,
                file_location=file_location,
            )
        )
    return GraphDocumentBlobRegistrationResult(registered=registered, skipped=skipped)


def list_eligible_document_blob_refs(
    *,
    run_id: str,
    question_id: str,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
) -> list[EligibleDocumentBlobRef]:
    normalized_run_id = _clean_required(run_id, field_name="run_id")
    normalized_question_id = _clean_required(question_id, field_name="question_id")
    database = ensure_database_schema(storage_config)
    with database.session_scope() as session:
        decision = session.get(
            FS11QCGateDecisionRow,
            {"run_id": normalized_run_id, "question_id": normalized_question_id},
        )
        if decision is None or str(decision.gate_status) != "pass":
            return []

        rows = session.scalars(
            select(InformationNodeRow)
            .join(NodeRow, NodeRow.node_id == InformationNodeRow.node_id)
            .join(EdgeRow, EdgeRow.from_node == InformationNodeRow.node_id)
            .join(ReferenceEdgeRow, ReferenceEdgeRow.edge_id == EdgeRow.edge_id)
            .where(
                NodeRow.run_id == normalized_run_id,
                NodeRow.question_id == normalized_question_id,
                EdgeRow.to_node.is_not(None),
                ReferenceEdgeRow.reference_type == "evidence",
            )
            .order_by(InformationNodeRow.node_id)
        ).all()
        eligible: list[EligibleDocumentBlobRef] = []
        seen_nodes: set[str] = set()
        for info in rows:
            if str(info.node_id) in seen_nodes:
                continue
            seen_nodes.add(str(info.node_id))
            locator = dict(info.file_location or {})
            if locator.get("artifact_kind") != DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND or locator.get("storage_kind") != "artifact":
                continue
            artifact_id = str(locator.get("artifact_id") or "").strip()
            if not artifact_id:
                continue
            artifact = session.get(ArtifactRow, artifact_id)
            if artifact is None or str(artifact.kind) != DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND:
                continue
            if str(locator.get("sha256") or "") != str(artifact.sha256):
                continue
            artifact_run_id = str(artifact.run_id) if artifact.run_id is not None else normalized_run_id
            artifact_question_id = str(artifact.question_id) if artifact.question_id is not None else normalized_question_id
            if artifact_run_id != normalized_run_id or artifact_question_id != normalized_question_id:
                continue
            eligible.append(
                EligibleDocumentBlobRef(
                    node_id=str(info.node_id),
                    artifact_id=str(artifact.artifact_id),
                    object_key=str(artifact.object_key),
                    sha256=str(artifact.sha256),
                    content_type=str(artifact.content_type),
                    source_url=str(locator.get("source_url")) if locator.get("source_url") is not None else None,
                    regeneration=dict(locator.get("regeneration") or {}),
                    file_location=locator,
                )
            )
    return eligible


def build_fs10_document_replay_payloads(
    *,
    run_id: str,
    question_id: str,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    payloads: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for ref in list_eligible_document_blob_refs(run_id=run_id, question_id=question_id, storage_config=storage_config):
        locator = dict(ref.file_location)
        payloads.append(
            {
                "schema_version": "fs10_document_replay_ref_v1",
                "run_id": _clean_required(run_id, field_name="run_id"),
                "question_id": _clean_required(question_id, field_name="question_id"),
                "upstream_fs": "FS2",
                "document_producing_step": "STEP_2A-doc-or-STEP_4",
                "information_node_id": ref.node_id,
                "artifact_id": ref.artifact_id,
                "artifact_kind": DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND,
                "object_key": ref.object_key,
                "sha256": ref.sha256,
                "content_type": ref.content_type,
                "size_bytes": locator.get("size_bytes"),
                "source_url": ref.source_url,
                "retrieval_source_id": locator.get("retrieval_source_id"),
                "retrieved_at": locator.get("retrieved_at"),
                "regeneration": dict(ref.regeneration),
                "fs11_concern_refs": list(locator.get("fs11_concern_refs") or []),
            }
        )
    return payloads


__all__ = [
    "DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND",
    "EligibleDocumentBlobRef",
    "GraphDocumentBlobRegistrationResult",
    "RegisteredGraphDocumentBlob",
    "StoredDocumentBlob",
    "attach_document_blob_to_information_node",
    "build_fs10_document_replay_payloads",
    "build_document_file_location",
    "list_eligible_document_blob_refs",
    "register_fs2_graph_document_blobs",
    "register_document_blob_artifact",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from packages.storage.database import (
    ArtifactRow,
    Base,
    EdgeRow,
    EdgeType,
    FS11QCGateDecisionRow,
    FS11QCRunRow,
    GraphRow,
    InformationNodeRow,
    NodeRow,
    NodeType,
    QuestionRow,
    QuestionType,
    ReferenceEdgeRow,
    ReferenceKind,
    RunRow,
    RunStatus,
    TargetRow,
    initialize_database_schema,
)
from packages.storage.document_store import (
    DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND,
    attach_document_blob_to_information_node,
    build_fs10_document_replay_payloads,
    list_eligible_document_blob_refs,
    register_document_blob_artifact,
    register_fs2_graph_document_blobs,
)
from packages.storage.runtime_config import StorageRuntimeConfig

SHA = "a" * 64


def _storage_config(tmp_path: Path) -> StorageRuntimeConfig:
    return StorageRuntimeConfig(
        database_url=f"sqlite+pysqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'document_store.db').as_posix()}",
        local_workspace_root=tmp_path / "workspace",
        project_root=tmp_path,
    )


def test_document_blob_uses_artifact_and_information_node_locator_authority(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    storage_config = _storage_config(tmp_path)
    database = initialize_database_schema(storage_config)

    stored = register_document_blob_artifact(
        artifact_id="artifact_doc_1",
        object_key="object://docs/doc-1.pdf",
        content_type="application/pdf",
        sha256=SHA,
        size_bytes=1234,
        run_id="run_doc",
        question_id="q_doc",
        source_url="https://example.test/doc-1.pdf",
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )

    assert stored.artifact_id == "artifact_doc_1"
    assert stored.file_location["storage_kind"] == "artifact"
    assert stored.file_location["artifact_kind"] == DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND
    assert stored.file_location["artifact_id"] == "artifact_doc_1"
    assert stored.file_location["sha256"] == SHA

    with database.session_scope() as session:
        artifact = session.get(ArtifactRow, "artifact_doc_1")
        assert artifact is not None
        assert artifact.kind == DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND
        assert artifact.object_key == "object://docs/doc-1.pdf"


def _seed_graph_with_document(storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig) -> None:
    database = initialize_database_schema(storage_config)
    with database.session_scope() as session:
        session.add(
            QuestionRow(
                question_id="q_doc",
                title="Will the document be eligible?",
                type=QuestionType.BINARY,
                targets={"options": ["yes", "no"]},
                source="test",
            )
        )
        session.add(RunRow(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", status=RunStatus.RUNNING))
        session.add(GraphRow(graph_id="graph_doc", run_id="run_doc"))
        session.add(
            NodeRow(
                node_id="target_doc",
                graph_id="graph_doc",
                type=NodeType.TARGET,
                question_id="q_doc",
                run_id="run_doc",
                label="Target",
            )
        )
        session.add(TargetRow(node_id="target_doc"))
        session.add(
            NodeRow(
                node_id="info_doc",
                graph_id="graph_doc",
                type=NodeType.INFORMATION,
                question_id="q_doc",
                run_id="run_doc",
                label="Document evidence",
            )
        )
        session.add(InformationNodeRow(node_id="info_doc", file_location={}, content_type="application/pdf"))
        session.add(
            EdgeRow(
                edge_id="edge_doc",
                graph_id="graph_doc",
                from_node="info_doc",
                to_node="target_doc",
                type=EdgeType.REFERENCE,
            )
        )
        session.add(ReferenceEdgeRow(edge_id="edge_doc", reference_type=ReferenceKind.EVIDENCE))
        session.add(FS11QCRunRow(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", generated_at="2026-05-06T00:00:00Z"))
        session.add(FS11QCGateDecisionRow(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", gate_status="pass"))

    register_document_blob_artifact(
        artifact_id="artifact_doc_1",
        object_key="object://docs/doc-1.pdf",
        content_type="application/pdf",
        sha256=SHA,
        size_bytes=1234,
        run_id="run_doc",
        question_id="q_doc",
        regeneration={"artifact_id": "artifact_doc_1"},
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )
    attach_document_blob_to_information_node(
        node_id="info_doc",
        artifact_id="artifact_doc_1",
        regeneration={"artifact_id": "artifact_doc_1"},
        fs11_concern_refs=[{"run_id": "run_doc", "question_id": "q_doc", "concern_id": "concern_1"}],
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )


def test_document_eligibility_requires_evidence_edge_fs11_pass_and_sha_match(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    storage_config = _storage_config(tmp_path)
    _seed_graph_with_document(storage_config)

    eligible = list_eligible_document_blob_refs(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", storage_config=storage_config)
    assert [row.artifact_id for row in eligible] == ["artifact_doc_1"]
    assert eligible[0].file_location["fs11_concern_refs"] == [{"run_id": "run_doc", "question_id": "q_doc", "concern_id": "concern_1"}]

    database = initialize_database_schema(storage_config)
    with database.session_scope() as session:
        decision = session.get(FS11QCGateDecisionRow, {"run_id": "run_doc", "question_id": "q_doc"})
        assert decision is not None
        decision.gate_status = "blocked"
    assert list_eligible_document_blob_refs(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", storage_config=storage_config) == []

    with database.session_scope() as session:
        decision = session.get(FS11QCGateDecisionRow, {"run_id": "run_doc", "question_id": "q_doc"})
        assert decision is not None
        decision.gate_status = "pass"
        node = session.get(InformationNodeRow, "info_doc")
        assert node is not None
        locator = dict(node.file_location)
        locator["sha256"] = "b" * 64
        node.file_location = locator
    assert list_eligible_document_blob_refs(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", storage_config=storage_config) == []


def test_non_regenerable_document_blob_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    storage_config = _storage_config(tmp_path)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="source_url or a stable"):
        register_document_blob_artifact(
            artifact_id="artifact_doc_1",
            object_key="object://docs/doc-1.pdf",
            content_type="application/pdf",
            sha256=SHA,
            size_bytes=1234,
            storage_config=storage_config,
        )


def test_fs2_graph_document_blob_registration_wires_file_location_and_replay_payload(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    storage_config = _storage_config(tmp_path)
    database = initialize_database_schema(storage_config)
    document_path = tmp_path / "workspace" / "evidence" / "policy-report.pdf"
    document_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    document_path.write_bytes(b"policy report bytes")

    with database.session_scope() as session:
        session.add(
            QuestionRow(
                question_id="q_doc",
                title="Will the document be eligible?",
                type=QuestionType.BINARY,
                targets={"options": ["yes", "no"]},
                source="test",
            )
        )
        session.add(RunRow(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", status=RunStatus.RUNNING))
        session.add(GraphRow(graph_id="graph_doc", run_id="run_doc"))
        session.add(
            NodeRow(
                node_id="target_doc",
                graph_id="graph_doc",
                type=NodeType.TARGET,
                question_id="q_doc",
                run_id="run_doc",
                label="Target",
            )
        )
        session.add(TargetRow(node_id="target_doc"))
        session.add(
            NodeRow(
                node_id="info_doc",
                graph_id="graph_doc",
                type=NodeType.INFORMATION,
                question_id="q_doc",
                run_id="run_doc",
                label="Policy report",
            )
        )
        session.add(InformationNodeRow(node_id="info_doc", file_location={}, content_type="application/pdf"))
        session.add(
            EdgeRow(
                edge_id="edge_doc",
                graph_id="graph_doc",
                from_node="info_doc",
                to_node="target_doc",
                type=EdgeType.REFERENCE,
            )
        )
        session.add(ReferenceEdgeRow(edge_id="edge_doc", reference_type=ReferenceKind.EVIDENCE))
        session.add(FS11QCRunRow(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", generated_at="2026-05-06T00:00:00Z"))
        session.add(FS11QCGateDecisionRow(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", gate_status="pass"))

    graph = SimpleNamespace(
        nodes={
            "info_doc": SimpleNamespace(
                id="info_doc",
                type="evidence",
                label="Policy report",
                data={
                    "question_id": "q_doc",
                    "detail": {
                        "document_link": str(document_path),
                        "document_key": "policy_report",
                        "filename": "policy-report.pdf",
                    },
                    "provenance": {
                        "source_kind": "downloaded_literature",
                        "sources": [
                            {
                                "provider": "policy_api",
                                "entry_id": "policy-report",
                                "pdf_url": "https://example.test/policy-report.pdf",
                                "local_path": str(document_path),

                        ],
                    },

            )

    result = register_fs2_graph_document_blobs(
        graph=graph,
        run_id="run_doc",
        question_id="q_doc",
        storage_config=storage_config,
        strict=True,
    )

    assert len(result.registered) == 1
    assert result.skipped == []
    registered = result.registered[0]
    assert registered.node_id == "info_doc"
    assert registered.content_type == "application/pdf"
    assert registered.source_url == "https://example.test/policy-report.pdf"

    with database.session_scope() as session:
        artifact = session.get(ArtifactRow, registered.artifact_id)
        info = session.get(InformationNodeRow, "info_doc")
        assert artifact is not None
        assert artifact.kind == DOCUMENT_BLOB_KIND
        assert info is not None
        assert info.file_location["artifact_id"] == registered.artifact_id
        assert info.file_location["object_key"] == str(document_path)
        assert info.file_location["retrieval_source_id"] == "policy_api:policy-report"
        assert (info.detail or {})["detail"]["file_location"]["artifact_id"] == registered.artifact_id
        retrieved_at = info.file_location["retrieved_at"]

    eligible = list_eligible_document_blob_refs(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", storage_config=storage_config)
    assert [item.artifact_id for item in eligible] == [registered.artifact_id]

    replay_payloads = build_fs10_document_replay_payloads(run_id="run_doc", question_id="q_doc", storage_config=storage_config)
    assert replay_payloads == [

            "schema_version": "fs10_document_replay_ref_v1",

            "retrieved_at": retrieved_at,
            "regeneration": {

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from packages.domain.graph_entity_validation import (
    CANONICAL_NODE_TYPES,
    VALID_CONDITIONAL_ENDPOINTS,
    VALID_REFERENCE_ENDPOINTS,
    validate_graph_entities,
)
from packages.storage import EdgeRecord, NodeRecord
from packages.storage.database import EdgeType, NodeType, ReferenceKind

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPORT_PATH = REPO_ROOT / "docs/plans/definitions/graph-entity-conformance-report.md"


def _read(path: str) -> str:
    return (REPO_ROOT / path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def _json(path: str) -> dict:
    return json.loads(_read(path))


def _issue_codes(nodes: list[NodeRecord], edges: list[EdgeRecord], *, question_id: str) -> set[str]:
    return {
        issue.code
        for issue in validate_graph_entities(
            nodes=nodes,
            edges=edges,
            question_ids=[question_id],
            graph_kind="full",
        )
    }


def test_validator_and_database_enums_match_canonical_graph_entities() -> None:
    assert CANONICAL_NODE_TYPES == {"target", "driver", "indicator", "information"}
    assert {item.value for item in NodeType} == CANONICAL_NODE_TYPES
    assert {item.value for item in EdgeType} == {"conditional", "reference"}
    assert VALID_CONDITIONAL_ENDPOINTS == {
        ("indicator", "driver"),
        ("indicator", "target"),
        ("driver", "driver"),
        ("driver", "target"),
    }
    assert VALID_REFERENCE_ENDPOINTS == {
        ("information", "indicator"),
        ("information", "driver"),
    }


def test_validator_rejects_database_historic_data_reference_kind() -> None:
    assert "historic_data" in {item.value for item in ReferenceKind}
    nodes = [
        NodeRecord(node_id="info", type="information", label="Source", toi=None, data={"question_id": "q1"}),
        NodeRecord(node_id="indicator", type="indicator", label="Indicator", toi=None, data={"question_id": "q1"}),
    ]
    edges = [
        EdgeRecord(
            edge_id="historic_reference",
            from_node="info",
            to_node="indicator",
            toi=None,
            type="REFERENCE",
            data={"reference_type": "historic_data"},
        ),
    ]

    assert _issue_codes(nodes, edges, question_id="q1") == {"invalid_reference_kind"}


def test_api_golden_graph_output_drift_is_recorded_in_report() -> None:
    payload = _json("tests/golden/graph_bundle.json")
    nodes = [
        NodeRecord(
            node_id=str(row["id"]),
            type=str(row["type"]),
            label=str(row.get("label") or ""),
            toi=row.get("toi"),
            baseline_p=row.get("baseline_p"),
            posterior_p=row.get("posterior_p"),
            data=dict(row.get("data") or {}),
        )
        for row in payload["nodes"]
    ]
    edges = [
        EdgeRecord(
            edge_id=str(row["id"]),
            from_node=str(row["from_id"]),
            to_node=str(row["to_id"]),
            toi=row.get("toi"),
            weight=row.get("weight"),
            type=str(row.get("type") or ""),
            data=dict(row.get("data") or {}),
        )
        for row in payload["edges"]
    ]

    assert "driver_target_reference_edge" in _issue_codes(nodes, edges, question_id="reg_q_parent")
    report = REPORT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "tests/golden/graph_bundle.json" in report
    assert "driver_target_reference_edge" in report


def test_conformance_report_records_current_cross_layer_blockers() -> None:
    report = REPORT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    schema = _json("core/schemas/graph.schema.json")
    node_enum = set(schema["properties"]["nodes"]["items"]["properties"]["type"]["enum"])
    edge_enum = set(schema["properties"]["edges"]["items"]["properties"]["type"]["enum"])
    if (node_enum - CANONICAL_NODE_TYPES) or (CANONICAL_NODE_TYPES - node_enum) or "INSTANTIATES" in edge_enum:
        assert "core/schemas/graph.schema.json" in report
        for marker in ["question", "evidence", "evidence_price_data", "historic_event_set", "INSTANTIATES", "information"]:
            assert marker in report

    if "historic_data" in {item.value for item in ReferenceKind}:
        assert "ReferenceKind.HISTORIC_DATA" in report
        assert "docs/plans/database/proposed-database-schema.md" in report

    repository_text = _read("packages/storage/sql_repositories.py")
    if "return _NODE_TYPE_MAP.get(raw_type, NodeType.DRIVER)" in repository_text:
        assert "_node_type_for_record" in report
    if 'EdgeType.CONDITIONAL if raw_type == "CONDITIONAL" else EdgeType.REFERENCE' in repository_text:
        assert "_edge_record_to_row" in report

    theme_text = _read("frontend/theme.js")
    cytoscape_text = _read("frontend/cytoscape-styles.js")
    if "evidence:" in theme_text or "option:" in theme_text or 'node[type="evidence_group"]' in cytoscape_text:
        assert "frontend/theme.js" in report
        assert "frontend/cytoscape-styles.js" in report

    assert "P-E2E-GRAPH-01" in report
```

---

## SPI-227 Phase 2: Harmonization — dev_sprint vs design docs vs dev

**Source artifacts:** `docs/plans/shared_architecture/document-storage-design.md`, `docs/plans/FS2/fs2-fs10-fs11-integration-design.md`, SPI-227 Linear ticket description.

### Design-implementation alignment

The dev_sprint `document_store.py` is well-aligned with the design docs. All key invariants check out:

| Design requirement | dev_sprint implementation | Status |
|---|---|---|
| No `document_blobs` table | Uses `artifacts` table with `kind='document_blob'` | Correct |
| `information_nodes.file_location` as locator | Writes `information_file_location_v1` schema | Correct |
| SHA256 integrity | 64-char hex validation, cross-checked on eligibility | Correct |
| FS11 gate enforcement | `list_eligible_document_blob_refs` checks `gate_status='pass'` | Correct |
| FS10 replay payloads | `fs10_document_replay_ref_v1` schema | Correct |
| Regeneration requirement | Rejects blobs without `source_url` or durable ref | Correct |
| DB-only (no JSON files as SoR) | All state in SQLAlchemy rows | Correct |

No contradictions or overcomplications found.

### What already exists on dev (no changes needed)

- `ArtifactRow`, `InformationNodeRow`, `NodeRow`, `EdgeRow`, `ReferenceEdgeRow`, `FS11QCGateDecisionRow` — all present
- `packages/storage/__init__.py` — already wires `document_store` imports via try/except
- `packages/storage/runtime_config.py` — present
- `packages/domain/qc/research_defensibility_store.py` — present
- `tests/test_artifact_repository_contract.py` — identical on both branches
- `tests/test_storage_repositories.py` — exists (document-storage tests don't depend on its changes)

### Files to create

1. **`packages/storage/document_store.py`** (~500 lines) — port directly from dev_sprint
2. **`tests/test_document_storage_authority.py`** (~250 lines) — port directly from dev_sprint, 5 tests covering all acceptance criteria

### Dependency gap: `test_graph_entity_conformance.py`

The acceptance criteria list `tests/test_graph_entity_conformance.py`, but it imports `packages/domain/graph_entity_validation.py` which **does not exist on dev**. This module is a graph validation helper — more naturally scoped to SPI-232 (FS3 extraction/graph admission) or SPI-228 (graph gate repair).

**Proposal:** Defer `test_graph_entity_conformance.py` to SPI-232. Call it out explicitly in the PR under "Out of scope (deliberately)".

### Proposed approach

1. Branch off `dev`
2. Create `packages/storage/document_store.py` — direct port from dev_sprint (no modifications needed)
3. Create `tests/test_document_storage_authority.py` — direct port from dev_sprint
4. No changes to `database.py`, `sql_repositories.py`, or `__init__.py` (everything needed already exists)
5. Run all acceptance criteria checks
6. Open draft PR to `dev`, tag @kungfusaini
7. Mark `test_graph_entity_conformance.py` as out-of-scope, deferred to SPI-232

### Open question

Should I also port `graph_entity_validation.py` + `test_graph_entity_conformance.py` as part of this ticket, or defer to SPI-232? The ticket acceptance criteria lists the conformance test, but its dependency doesn't exist on dev and is graph-domain code, not storage/document code.

def _sync_fs2_document_blob_authority(
    *,
    graph: Any,
    run_id: str,
    question_id: str,
    storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    storage_config = getattr(storage_adapters, "storage_runtime_config", None)
    if storage_config is None:
        return {
            "schema_version": "fs2_document_blob_registration_v1",
            "status": "skipped",
            "reason": "no_storage_config",
            "registered_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": 0,
        }
    result = register_fs2_graph_document_blobs(
        graph=graph,
        run_id=run_id,
        question_id=question_id,
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )
    payload = result.to_payload()
    payload["status"] = "ok"
    return payload


def _fs2_runtime_research_source_refs(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    refs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node in (getattr(graph, "nodes", {}) or {}).values():
        node_type = str(getattr(node, "type", "") or "").strip().lower()
        if node_type not in {"evidence", "information"}:
            continue
        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
        if not isinstance(data, Mapping):
            continue

> AGENT

Here's the real picture:

**`document_store.py` has exactly one caller on dev_sprint** — the main research pipeline (`services/worker/research_driver_question_pipeline.py`). It's called at two points:

1. After `graph.save()` during the priors/baseline stage (step 6b)
2. After `graph.save()` during the probability inference stage

Both calls do the same thing: `_sync_fs2_document_blob_authority()` which calls `register_fs2_graph_document_blobs()` to scan the persisted graph for evidence/information nodes, hash their file bytes, register them as `ArtifactRow(kind='document_blob')`, and wire `information_nodes.file_location` with the artifact-backed locator.

**The pipeline on dev does NOT call this yet** — it doesn't reference `document_store` at all. So you're right to probe: this module is infrastructure that the pipeline will eventually use, but the pipeline integration itself isn't part of SPI-227's scope. The pipeline changes would come when the pipeline is ported (probably SPI-232 or a later ticket).

**What SPI-227 gives you concretely:**
- The storage-layer building block (register, attach, query eligibility, build replay payloads)
- Tests proving the contract works
- The `__init__.py` already wires the imports, so downstream callers can use it once they land

**What it doesn't give you yet:**
- Anything actually calling it at runtime — that requires pipeline changes from a later ticket

So it's a leaf dependency being landed early so downstream tickets (SPI-232, the pipeline port) can use it. Does that framing make sense, or would you rather bundle this with the ticket that actually wires it into the pipeline?

6	+# Document Storage Design
7	+
8	+> **Source artifact**: design review session and reconciliation plan,
9	+> 2026-05-06.
10	+> **Related source artifacts**: [FS2 planned architecture](../FS2/fs2-planned-architecture.md), [FS10 planned architecture](../FS10/fs10-planned-architecture.md), [FS11 planned architecture](../FS11/fs11-planned-architecture.md), [Proposed Database Schema](proposed-database-schema.md), [Graph entity definitions](graph-entity-definitions.md), [FS diagram schema](../../../agent_rules/fs-diagram-schema.md), [packages/storage/database.py](../../../packages/storage/database.py), and [packages/domain/qc/research_defensibility_store.py](../../../packages/domain/qc/research_defensibility_store.py).
11	+> **Source artifact for capability-preserving reconciliation**: user request on 2026-05-07 to reconcile active design docs only while excluding audits and archive docs as current source material.
12	+> **Change type**: semantic.
13	+> **Canonical status**: this file is the canonical design reference for
14	+> artifact-backed document bytes and document locator metadata that FS2 produces
15	+> and FS3 / FS10 / FS11 consume.
16	+> **Storage discipline note**: this reference is a design proposal and does not
17	+> itself add migrations, create product persistence, or approve file-backed
18	+> durable storage. Any implementation must go through separately reviewed storage
19	+> design work, use approved DB-backed authority for durable Elijah product data,
20	+> and document deviations from this proposal explicitly. Per
21	+> [AGENTS.md](../../../AGENTS.md) lines 74-89, JSON files and local/object bytes
22	+> are caches, mirrors, exports, or artifact bytes; they are not authoritative
23	+> product-data stores.
24	+
25	+## Context
26	+
27	+FS2 needs a stable surface for downloaded reports, PDFs, HTML pages, and
28	+transformed document payloads that FS3 can chunk, FS10 can freeze for replay,
29	+and FS11 can review. The source docs already define two relevant authorities:
30	+
31	+- `artifacts` already carries byte-handle metadata: `kind`, `object_key`,
32	+  `content_type`, `sha256`, `size_bytes`, `run_id`, `job_id`, and
33	+  `question_id`.
34	+- `information_nodes.file_location` is an existing `JSON NOT NULL` locator for
35	+  the information object represented by a graph node.
36	+
37	+The intended outcome is to close the document persistence gap without inventing
38	+parallel byte tables, status vocabularies, or opaque concern references.
39	+
40	+## Design Summary
41	+
42	+**Document bytes use `artifacts`; document graph authority uses
43	+`information_nodes`.**
44	+
45	+- Document bytes are registered as `artifacts.kind='document_blob'`.
46	+- `information_nodes.file_location` remains the document locator authority and
47	+  stores the artifact-backed reference shape.
48	+- FS3 consumption eligibility is derived from FS11 DB authority, not from a
49	+  document-local status column.
50	+- FS11 concern links reference real `fs11_qc_concerns` rows by
51	+  `(run_id, question_id, concern_id)`.
52	+- FS10 frozen replay records must carry stable document and prompt references in
53	+  `fs10_validation_component_artifacts.payload_json`; replay must not depend on
54	+  a garbage-collectable local path alone.
55	+
56	+## Object Hierarchy
57	+
58	+```mermaid
59	+flowchart TB
60	+    RS["retrieval_sources<br/>source_id, provider,<br/>source_type, status"] --> A[("artifacts<br/>kind='document_blob', object_key,<br/>content_type, sha256, size_bytes")]
61	+    R["runs<br/>run_id"] --> A
62	+    A --> B[/"artifact bytes<br/>local workspace or object store"/]
63	+    A --> IN[("information_nodes<br/>node_id, file_location JSON,<br/>source, content_type")]
64	+    IN --> RE[("reference_edges<br/>reference_type='evidence'")]
65	+    RE --> N["driver / indicator<br/>nodes"]
66	+    FS11[("fs11_qc_gate_decisions<br/>gate_status, review_state")] --> FS3["FS3 chunker<br/>eligible document reader"]
67	+    FS11C[("fs11_qc_concerns<br/>run_id, question_id, concern_id")] -.->|"concern refs"| IN
68	+    IN --> FS3
69	+```
70	+
71	+## Storage Contract
72	+
73	+### Artifact Row
74	+
75	+Document bytes use the existing `artifacts` table with these conventions:
76	+
77	+| Column | Required | Notes |
78	+|---|---:|---|
79	+| `kind` | yes | Literal `document_blob` |
80	+| `object_key` | yes | Local artifact key or object-store key for the bytes |
81	+| `content_type` | yes | MIME type, for example `application/pdf` or `text/html` |
82	+| `sha256` | yes | Hex digest of the bytes consumed by downstream readers |
83	+| `size_bytes` | yes | Byte count for budgets and sanity checks |
84	+| `run_id` | yes for runtime FS2 / FS3 writes | Producing run |
85	+| `job_id` | optional | Batch context when available |
86	+| `question_id` | yes for question-scoped research | Question that caused retrieval or transform |
87	+
88	+The artifact row is the DB-backed byte handle. The bytes behind `object_key` are
89	+artifact bytes: they may live on local storage in dev and object storage in
90	+production, but product decisions must resolve through DB rows and replay
91	+payloads, not bare filesystem paths.
92	+
93	+### Information Node Locator
94	+
95	+`information_nodes.file_location` remains the canonical locator for the
96	+document represented by an information node. For artifact-backed documents, use
97	+this shape:
98	+
99	+```json
```

423	+def _clean_required(value: str | None, *, field_name: str) -> str:
424	+    cleaned = str(value or "").strip()
425	+    if not cleaned:
426	+        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} is required")
427	+    return cleaned
428	+
429	+
430	+def _clean_optional(value: str | None) -> str | None:
431	+    cleaned = str(value or "").strip()
432	+    return cleaned or None
433	+
434	+
435	+def _clean_sha256(value: str | None) -> str:
436	+    cleaned = _clean_required(value, field_name="sha256")
437	+    if len(cleaned) != 64:
438	+        raise ValueError("sha256 must be a 64-character hex digest")
439	+    return cleaned
440	+
441	+
442	+def _has_durable_ref(*, source_url: str | None, regeneration: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
443	+    if _clean_optional(source_url):
444	+        return True
445	+    return any(bool(regeneration.get(key)) for key in _DURABLE_REF_KEYS)
446	+
447	+
448	+def _validate_regeneration(*, source_url: str | None, regeneration: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
449	+    if _has_durable_ref(source_url=source_url, regeneration=regeneration):
450	+        return
451	+    raise ValueError("document blobs require source_url or a stable DB/API/artifact regeneration reference")
452	+
453	+
454	+def _validate_fs11_refs(refs: list[dict[str, Any]] | None) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
455	+    normalized: list[dict[str, str]] = []
456	+    for item in list(refs or []):
457	+        if not isinstance(item, dict):
458	+            raise ValueError("fs11_concern_refs must be JSON objects")
459	+        normalized.append(

462	+                "question_id": _clean_required(str(item.get("question_id") or ""), field_name="fs11_concern_refs.question_id"),
463	+                "concern_id": _clean_required(str(item.get("concern_id") or ""), field_name="fs11_concern_refs.concern_id"),
464	+            }
465	+        )
466	+    return normalized
467	+

484	+    return {

492	+        "size_bytes": int(size_bytes) if size_bytes is not None else None,
493	+        "source_url": _clean_optional(source_url),
494	+        "retrieval_source_id": _clean_optional(retrieval_source_id),
495	+        "retrieved_at": _clean_optional(retrieved_at),
496	+        "regeneration": regeneration_json,
497	+        "fs11_concern_refs": _validate_fs11_refs(fs11_concern_refs),
498	+    }

502	+    return StoredDocumentBlob(
