1	"""
2	LLM tool for proposing sub-question targets when promoting a driver.
3	
4	Given a driver's context and its parent question, proposes a structured
5	set of MECE outcome options suitable for a standalone sub-question.
6	
7	Where It Is Used:
8	- services.api.routes.driver_promotion
9	
10	Key Symbols:
11	- DriverPromotionProposalTool
12	- propose_driver_promotion
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import logging
18	from typing import Any
19	
20	from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
21	
22	from packages.inference.llm import BaseLLMTool, LLMConfig
23	
24	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
25	
26	
27	_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
28	You are a forecasting question designer. Given a causal driver from a \
29	Bayesian forecasting graph, propose how to decompose it into a standalone \
30	sub-question with mutually exclusive, collectively exhaustive (MECE) outcome \
31	options.
32	
33	Rules:
34	- Propose 2-7 outcome options that cover the full range of possibilities.
35	- For quantitative drivers (rates, prices, percentages), prefer numeric \
36	brackets with clear boundaries.
37	- For event-based drivers, prefer categorical options or binary yes/no.
38	- Each option must be unambiguous and non-overlapping.
39	- The question label should be a clear forecasting question, not a \
40	statement.
41	- Suggested baseline probabilities should sum to approximately 1.0."""
42	
43	
44	class _ProposedOption(BaseModel):
45	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
46	
47	    label: str = Field(description="Outcome option label")
48	    description: str = Field(default="", description="What this outcome means")
49	    suggested_baseline_p: float | None = Field(
50	        default=None,
51	        description="Suggested prior probability [0.01, 0.99]",
52	    )
53	
54	
55	class _DriverPromotionLLMOutput(BaseModel):
56	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
57	
58	    question_label: str = Field(description="Proposed question text")
59	    classification: str = Field(
60	        description="One of: binary, categorical, numeric_bracket",
61	    )
62	    options: list[_ProposedOption] = Field(
63	        description="2-7 MECE outcome options",
64	    )
65	    rationale: str = Field(default="", description="Why this decomposition makes sense")
66	
67	
68	class DriverPromotionProposalTool(BaseLLMTool[dict[str, Any]]):
69	    response_model = _DriverPromotionLLMOutput
70	
71	    def __init__(self, *, model: str | None = None) -> None:
72	        model_text = str(model or "").strip()
73	        super().__init__(
74	            system_prompt=_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
75	            temperature=0.3,
76	            max_output_tokens=2000,
77	            llm=LLMConfig(model=model_text, task_id="FS8.driver_promotion"),
78	            cache_enabled=True,
79	        )
80	
81	    def build_messages(self, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
82	        driver_label = kwargs.get("driver_label", "")
83	        driver_description = kwargs.get("driver_description", "")
84	        driver_mechanism = kwargs.get("driver_mechanism", "")
85	        parent_question_label = kwargs.get("parent_question_label", "")
86	        parent_options = kwargs.get("parent_options", [])
87	        parent_classification = kwargs.get("parent_classification", "")
88	        parent_domain = kwargs.get("parent_domain", "")
89	        parent_closing_date = kwargs.get("parent_closing_date", "")
90	        edge_judgments = kwargs.get("edge_judgments", [])
91	
92	        parts = [
93	            f"PARENT QUESTION: {parent_question_label}",
94	            f"Parent classification: {parent_classification}",
95	            f"Parent options: {', '.join(str(o) for o in parent_options)}",
96	            f"Domain: {parent_domain}",
97	            f"Closing date: {parent_closing_date}",
98	            "",
99	            f"DRIVER TO PROMOTE: {driver_label}",
100	            f"Description: {driver_description}" if driver_description else "",
101	            f"Mechanism: {driver_mechanism}" if driver_mechanism else "",
102	        ]
103	        if edge_judgments:
104	            parts.append("")
105	            parts.append("Existing edge judgments (driver → parent targets):")
106	            for ej in edge_judgments:
107	                parts.append(f"  → {ej['target_label']}: {ej['judgment']}")
108	
109	        parts.append("")
110	        parts.append("Propose a standalone forecasting sub-question for this driver with MECE outcome options. Return structured JSON.")
111	
112	        return [{"role": "user", "content": "\n".join(parts)}]
113	
114	    def postprocess(self, result: Any, **_: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
115	        if hasattr(result, "model_dump"):
116	            return result.model_dump(mode="json")
117	        if isinstance(result, dict):
118	            return dict(result)
119	        return {}
120	
121	
122	def propose_driver_promotion(
123	    *,
124	    driver_label: str,
125	    driver_description: str,
126	    driver_mechanism: str,
127	    parent_question_label: str,
128	    parent_options: list[str],
129	    parent_classification: str,
130	    parent_domain: str,
131	    parent_closing_date: str,
132	    edge_judgments: list[dict[str, str]],
133	    model: str | None = None,
134	) -> dict[str, Any]:
135	    """Call the LLM to propose a sub-question structure for a driver.
136	
137	    Returns a dict with keys: question_label, classification, options, rationale.
138	    Returns a binary fallback if the LLM call fails.
139	    """
140	    tool = DriverPromotionProposalTool(model=model)
141	    try:
142	        result = tool.run(
143	            driver_label=driver_label,
144	            driver_description=driver_description,
145	            driver_mechanism=driver_mechanism,
146	            parent_question_label=parent_question_label,
147	            parent_options=parent_options,
148	            parent_classification=parent_classification,
149	            parent_domain=parent_domain,
150	            parent_closing_date=parent_closing_date,
151	            edge_judgments=edge_judgments,
152	        )
153	        if isinstance(result, dict) and result.get("options"):
154	            return result
155	    except Exception:
156	        logger.warning("LLM driver promotion proposal failed, using binary fallback", exc_info=True)
157	
158	    return {
159	        "question_label": f"Will '{driver_label}' materialise?",
160	        "classification": "binary",
161	        "options": [
162	            {"label": "Yes", "description": f"{driver_label} materialises", "suggested_baseline_p": 0.5},
163	            {"label": "No", "description": f"{driver_label} does not materialise", "suggested_baseline_p": 0.5},
164	        ],
165	        "rationale": "Binary fallback (LLM proposal unavailable).",
166	    }
167

68	        f"Return at most {max_indicators} indicators.\n\n"
69	        "Each indicator MUST include ALL of these fields:\n\n"
70	        "name: A short, specific, measurable observable. Valid examples: market data,\n"
71	        "  filings, macro prints, surveys, event counts, spreads, volumes, revisions.\n"
72	        "  Do NOT restate the driver as an indicator.\n\n"
73	        'direction: "supports" if the indicator rising/appearing confirms the driver.\n'
74	        '  "contradicts" if it disconfirms the driver.\n\n'
75	        "relationship_judgment: Select EXACTLY ONE from this list:\n"
76	        f"  A = driver '{driver_title}'\n"
77	        "  B = indicator candidate\n"
78	        "  Gradient labels (strength + sign):\n"
79	        "  - very low (for)\n"
80	        "  - low (for)\n"
81	        "  - med (for)\n"
82	        "  - high (for)\n"
83	        "  - very high (for)\n"
84	        "  - very low (against)\n"
85	        "  - low (against)\n"
86	        "  - med (against)\n"
87	        "  - high (against)\n"
88	        "  - very high (against)\n"
89	        "  Logical labels (sufficiency/necessity):\n"
90	        "  - B is sufficient for A\n"
91	        "  - \u00acB is sufficient for A\n"
92	        "  - B is sufficient for \u00acA\n"
93	        "  - \u00acB is sufficient for \u00acA\n"
94	        "  - B is necessary for A\n"
95	        "  - \u00acB is necessary for A\n"
96	        "  - B is necessary for \u00acA\n"
97	        "  - \u00acB is necessary for \u00acA\n"
98	        "  Base your choice on the literature evidence, not generic priors.\n\n"
99	        "relationship_description: EXACTLY 2-3 sentences (maximum 80 words) that MUST contain ALL FIVE\n"
100	        "  of these elements:\n"
101	        "  (1) Direction \u2014 what happens to the driver likelihood when the indicator\n"
102	        "      moves (more likely / less likely)\n"
103	        "  (2) Conditions \u2014 under what circumstances the effect holds\n"
104	        "  (3) Timeframe \u2014 expected horizon (days, weeks, months)\n"
105	        "  (4) Mechanism \u2014 the causal channel connecting indicator to driver\n"
106	        "  (5) Target reference \u2014 which downstream targets or outcomes are affected\n"
107	        "      by the driver being confirmed or disconfirmed\n\n"
108	        "rationale: 1-2 sentences justifying this indicator-driver link, grounded in\n"
109	        "  the literature titles above.\n\n"
110	        "confidence: A float between 0.0 and 1.0 estimating how reliable this\n"
111	        "  indicator-driver link is based on the available evidence.\n\n"
112	        "Guidelines:\n"
113	        "- Indicators must be observable (not hypothetical states or opinions).\n"
114	        '- Include at least one "supports" indicator when possible.\n'
115	        '- Add "contradicts" indicators when plausible.\n'
116	        "- Keep names short and domain-specific.\n"
117	        "- Keep wording domain-agnostic unless the literature forces specificity.\n"
118	    )
119	
120	
121	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
122	# Pydantic models for structured output validation
123	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
124	
125	
126	class _IndicatorCandidate(BaseModel):
127	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
128	
129	    name: str
130	    direction: Literal["supports", "contradicts"] = "supports"
131	    relationship_judgment: str = ""
132	    relationship_description: str = ""
133	    rationale: str = ""
134	    confidence: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
135	
136	
137	class _IndicatorExtractionOutput(BaseModel):
138	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
139	
140	    indicators: list[_IndicatorCandidate] = Field(default_factory=list)
141	
142	
143	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
144	# 1. Input preparation
145	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
146	
147	
148	def prepare_extraction_prompt(
149	    *,
150	    driver_title: str,
151	    driver_query: str,
152	    source_titles: Sequence[str],
153	    max_indicators: int,
154	) -> tuple[str, str]:
155	    """Build the system and user prompts for indicator extraction.
156	
157	    Returns (system_prompt, user_prompt).
158	    """
159	    seen: set[str] = set()
160	    cleaned_titles: list[str] = []
161	    for t in source_titles:
162	        ct = clean_text(t, max_len=240)
163	        if ct and ct not in seen:
164	            seen.add(ct)
165	            cleaned_titles.append(ct)
166	    source_lines = "\n".join(f"- {t}" for t in cleaned_titles) if cleaned_titles else "- (none)"
167	    judgment_labels = "\n".join(f"  - {label}" for label in ALLOWED_RELATIONSHIP_JUDGMENTS)
168	    user_prompt = _build_user_prompt(
169	        driver_title=driver_title.strip(),
170	        driver_query=driver_query.strip(),
171	        source_lines=source_lines,
172	        max_indicators=max(1, int(max_indicators)),
173	        judgment_labels=judgment_labels,
174	    )
175	    return _SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt
176	
177	
178	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
179	# 2. Claude API call — the only place in this module that touches the API
180	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
181	
182	
183	def _get_litellm_completion_fn() -> Any:
184	    try:
185	        from litellm.main import completion
186	    except ImportError:
187	        raise ImportError("litellm package is required for indicator extraction.")
188	    return completion
189	
190	
191	def extract_causal_relationships(
192	    *,
193	    driver_title: str,
194	    driver_query: str,
195	    source_titles: Sequence[str],
196	    max_indicators: int,
197	) -> dict[str, Any]:
198	    """Call the LLM API (via LiteLLM/proxy) to extract indicator candidates for a single driver.
199	
200	    Returns the raw parsed JSON dict from the API response.
201	    Raises on API failure — callers must handle exceptions.
202	    """
203	    system_prompt, user_prompt = prepare_extraction_prompt(
204	        driver_title=driver_title,
205	        driver_query=driver_query,
206	        source_titles=source_titles,
207	        max_indicators=max_indicators,
208	    )
209	    completion_fn = _get_litellm_completion_fn()
210	
211	    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
212	        "model": resolve_task_model("FS3.indicator_extraction"),
213	        "messages": [
214	            {
215	                "role": "system",
216	                "content": system_prompt,
217	                **({} if os.environ.get("LITELLM_PROXY_BASE_URL") else {"cache_control": {"type": "ephemeral"}}),
218	            },
219	            {"role": "user", "content": user_prompt},
220	        ],
221	        "max_tokens": 1200,
222	        "temperature": 0.0,
223	    }
224	
225	    proxy_base = os.environ.get("LITELLM_PROXY_BASE_URL")
226	    if proxy_base:
227	        kwargs["api_base"] = proxy_base
228	        proxy_key = os.environ.get("LITELLM_API_KEY")
229	        if proxy_key:
230	            kwargs["api_key"] = proxy_key
231	
232	    response = completion_fn(**kwargs)
233	    raw_text = response.choices[0].message.content
234	    logger.debug("LiteLLM indicator extraction raw response: %s", raw_text)
235	
236	    return json.loads(raw_text)
237	
238	
239	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
240	# 3. Response validation
241	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
242	
243	
244	def validate_extraction_response(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
245	    """Validate the raw Claude JSON against the Pydantic schema.
246	
247	    Returns a list of indicator dicts (model_dump output).
248	    Returns an empty list if validation fails.
249	    """
250	    try:
251	        parsed = _IndicatorExtractionOutput.model_validate(raw)
252	        return [ind.model_dump(mode="json") for ind in parsed.indicators]
253	    except Exception as exc:
254	        logger.warning("Indicator extraction validation failed: %s", exc)
255	        return []
256	
257	
258	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
259	# 4. Graph-ready normalization (zero knowledge of Claude or the API)
260	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
261	
262	
263	def normalize_indicator_rows(
264	    *,
265	    rows_in: Sequence[object],
266	    driver_title: str,
267	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
268	    """Normalize raw indicator rows into graph-ready dicts.
269	
270	    Handles direction coercion, relationship_judgment normalization,
271	    confidence parsing, and metadata passthrough.
272	    """
273	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
274	    for item in rows_in:
275	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
276	            continue
277	        name = str(item.get("name") or "").strip()
278	        if not name:
279	            continue
280	        direction = str(item.get("direction") or "supports").strip().lower()
281	        if direction not in {"supports", "contradicts"}:
282	            direction = "supports"
283	        relationship_judgment = normalize_relationship_judgment(
284	            item.get("relationship_judgment"),
285	            fallback=relationship_judgment_from_direction(direction),
286	        )
287	        confidence = item.get("confidence")
288	        conf_val: float | None
289	        try:
290	            conf_val = float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None
291	        except Exception:
292	            conf_val = None
293	        relationship_description = str(item.get("relationship_description") or "").strip()
294	        rationale = clean_text(item.get("rationale"))
295	        row: dict[str, Any] = {
296	            "name": name,
297	            "direction": direction,
298	            "relationship_judgment": relationship_judgment,
299	            "relationship_description": relationship_description,
300	            "rationale": rationale,
301	            "confidence": conf_val,
302	        }
303	        for key in ("source_mode", "epistemic_status", "human_review_rationale", "origin_stage"):
304	            value = clean_text(item.get(key), max_len=240)
305	            if value:
306	                row[key] = value
307	        rows.append(row)
308	    return rows
309	
310	
311	def _mark_no_source_best_effort_indicators(
312	    rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
313	    *,
314	    driver_title: str,
315	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
316	    driver_label = clean_text(driver_title, max_len=120) or "this driver"
317	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
318	    for item in rows:
319	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
320	            continue
321	        row = dict(item)
322	        row["source_mode"] = "llm_best_effort_no_sources"
323	        row["epistemic_status"] = "hypothesis"
324	        row["human_review_rationale"] = (
325	            f"Best-effort indicator generated for '{driver_label}' without supporting literature sources. Review before relying on it."
326	        )
327	        row["origin_stage"] = "indicator_llm_best_effort"
328	        out.append(row)
329	    return out
330	
331	
332	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
333	# Compatibility wrapper — keeps the BaseLLMTool interface for run_tool_batch
334	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
335	
336	
337	class DriverIndicatorExtractionTool:
338	    """Indicator extraction tool with direct Claude API for single calls
339	    and BaseLLMTool-compatible interface for batch processing."""
340	
341	    response_model = _IndicatorExtractionOutput
342	    system_prompt = _SYSTEM_PROMPT
343	
344	    def __init__(self) -> None:
345	        self._model = resolve_task_model("FS3.indicator_extraction")
346	        from packages.inference.llm import LLMConfig, LLMPolicy
347	        from packages.inference.llm.runtime import LLMRuntime
348	
349	        self._runtime = LLMRuntime(
350	            llm=LLMConfig(model=self._model, task_id="FS3.indicator_extraction"),
351	            policy=LLMPolicy(
352	                role="tool",
353	                temperature=0.0,
354	                max_output_tokens=1200,
355	                enable_cache=True,
356	            ),
357	        )
358	
359	    @property
360	    def model(self) -> str:
361	        return self._runtime.model
362	
363	    @property
364	    def max_output_tokens(self) -> int:
365	        return int(self._runtime.max_output_tokens)
366	
367	    @max_output_tokens.setter
368	    def max_output_tokens(self, value: int) -> None:
369	        self._runtime.max_output_tokens = int(value)
370	
371	    def build_messages(
372	        self,
373	        *,
374	        driver_title: str,
375	        driver_query: str,
376	        source_titles: Sequence[str],
377	        max_indicators: int,
378	        **_: Any,
379	    ) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
380	        _, user_prompt = prepare_extraction_prompt(
381	            driver_title=driver_title,
382	            driver_query=driver_query,
383	            source_titles=source_titles,
384	            max_indicators=max_indicators,
385	        )
386	        return [{"role": "user", "content": user_prompt}]
387	
388	    def _build_full_message_list(self, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
389	        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
390	            {
391	                "role": "system",
392	                "content": self.system_prompt,
393	                **({} if os.environ.get("LITELLM_PROXY_BASE_URL") else {"cache_control": {"type": "ephemeral"}}),
394	            }
395	        ]
396	        messages.extend(self.build_messages(**kwargs))
397	        return messages
398	
399	    def get_response_model(self, **_: Any) -> type[Any] | None:
400	        return self.response_model
401	
402	    def postprocess(self, result: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
403	        if hasattr(result, "model_dump"):
404	            data = result.model_dump(mode="json")
405	        elif isinstance(result, dict):
406	            data = dict(result)
407	        else:
408	            data = {}
409	        driver_title = clean_text(kwargs.get("driver_title"))
410	        return normalize_indicator_rows(
411	            rows_in=data.get("indicators") or [],
412	            driver_title=driver_title,
413	        )
414	
415	    def run(self, **kwargs: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
416	        """Single-call path routed through the shared proxy-safe LLM runtime."""
417	        parsed = self._runtime.complete_structured(
418	            messages=self._build_full_message_list(**kwargs),
419	            response_model=self.response_model,
420	        )
421	        return self.postprocess(parsed, **kwargs)
422	
423	
424	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
425	# Public API — unchanged external interface
426	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
427	
428	
429	def extract_indicator_candidates_for_driver_payload(
430	    *,
431	    driver_title: str,
432	    driver_query: str,
433	    source_titles: Sequence[str],
434	    max_indicators: int = 4,
435	    llm_enabled: bool = True,
436	    fallback_pack_name: str = "generic_arxiv",
437	    precomputed_llm_result: Any = None,
438	) -> dict[str, Any]:
439	    pack = build_retrieval_pack_for_name(fallback_pack_name)
440	    cleaned_source_titles: list[str] = []
441	    for raw in source_titles:
442	        title = clean_text(raw, max_len=240)
443	        if title and title not in cleaned_source_titles:
444	            cleaned_source_titles.append(title)
445	    has_supporting_sources = bool(cleaned_source_titles)
446	
447	    llm_error = ""
448	    if llm_enabled:
449	        try:
450	            if precomputed_llm_result is not None:
451	                rows = precomputed_llm_result
452	            else:
453	                tool = DriverIndicatorExtractionTool()
454	                rows = tool.run(
455	                    driver_title=driver_title,
456	                    driver_query=driver_query,
457	                    source_titles=list(cleaned_source_titles),
458	                    max_indicators=max(1, int(max_indicators)),
459	                )
460	            if isinstance(rows, list):
461	                cleaned = normalize_indicator_rows(rows_in=rows, driver_title=driver_title)
462	                if cleaned:
463	                    if not has_supporting_sources:
464	                        cleaned = _mark_no_source_best_effort_indicators(cleaned, driver_title=driver_title)
465	                    return {
466	                        "indicators": cleaned[: max(1, int(max_indicators))],
467	                        "pack_name": pack.pack_name,
468	                        "fallback_policy": getattr(pack, "fallback_policy", ""),
469	                        "fallback_support": getattr(pack, "fallback_support", ""),
470	                        "fallback_used": False,
471	                        "review_required": not has_supporting_sources,
472	                        "message": (
473	                            ""
474	                            if has_supporting_sources
475	                            else "No supporting literature sources were available; returned best-effort LLM indicators."
476	                        ),
477	                        "llm_error": "",
478	                        "stage_outcome": build_stage_outcome(
479	                            status="ok" if has_supporting_sources else "partial",
480	                            reason_code=("indicator_llm_generated" if has_supporting_sources else "indicator_llm_best_effort_no_sources"),
481	                            message=(
482	                                "Indicator extraction returned usable LLM indicators."
483	                                if has_supporting_sources
484	                                else "No supporting literature sources were available; returned best-effort LLM indicators."
485	                            ),
486	                            fallback_used=False,
487	                            quality_flags=[] if has_supporting_sources else ["no_supporting_sources", "best_effort_only"],
488	                            extra={"indicator_count": len(cleaned[: max(1, int(max_indicators))])},
489	                        ),
490	                    }
491	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: PERF203
492	            llm_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
493	
494	    fallback_rows = normalize_indicator_rows(
495	        rows_in=pack.build_indicator_fallback(
496	            driver_title=driver_title,
497	            driver_query=driver_query,
498	            source_titles=list(cleaned_source_titles),
499	            max_indicators=max(1, int(max_indicators)),
500	            routing=None,
501	        ),
502	        driver_title=driver_title,
503	    )
504	    if fallback_rows:
505	        return {
506	            "indicators": fallback_rows[: max(1, int(max_indicators))],
507	            "pack_name": pack.pack_name,
508	            "fallback_policy": getattr(pack, "fallback_policy", ""),
509	            "fallback_support": getattr(pack, "fallback_support", ""),
510	            "fallback_used": True,
511	            "review_required": True,
512	            "message": f"Used pack-specific indicator fallback from pack '{pack.pack_name}'.",
513	            "llm_error": llm_error,
514	            "stage_outcome": build_stage_outcome(
515	                status="partial",
516	                reason_code="indicator_pack_fallback",
517	                message=f"Used pack-specific indicator fallback from pack '{pack.pack_name}'.",
518	                fallback_used=True,
519	                quality_flags=["pack_fallback_used"] + (["llm_exception"] if llm_error else []),
520	                extra={"indicator_count": len(fallback_rows[: max(1, int(max_indicators))]), "llm_error": llm_error},
521	            ),
522	        }
523	
524	    return {
525	        "indicators": [],
526	        "pack_name": pack.pack_name,
527	        "fallback_policy": getattr(pack, "fallback_policy", ""),
528	        "fallback_support": getattr(pack, "fallback_support", ""),
529	        "fallback_used": False,
530	        "review_required": True,
531	        "message": (
532	            f"No pack-specific fallback defined for indicators in pack '{pack.pack_name}'; no indicators were added automatically."
533	        ),
534	        "llm_error": llm_error,
535	        "stage_outcome": build_stage_outcome(
536	            status="failed",
537	            reason_code="indicator_llm_exception" if llm_error else "indicator_no_candidates",
538	            message=(
539	                f"No pack-specific fallback defined for indicators in pack '{pack.pack_name}'; no indicators were added automatically."
540	            ),
541	            fallback_used=False,
542	            quality_flags=["no_indicator_candidates"] + (["llm_exception"] if llm_error else []),
543	            extra={"indicator_count": 0, "llm_error": llm_error},
544	        ),
545	    }
546	
547	
548	def batch_extract_indicator_candidates(
549	    drivers: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
550	    *,
551	    max_indicators: int = 4,
552	    llm_enabled: bool = True,
553	    fallback_pack_name: str = "generic_arxiv",
554	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
555	    """Batch-extract indicator candidates for multiple drivers.
556	
557	    Uses the Anthropic Batch API when available, falling back to sequential.
558	    Each entry in ``drivers`` should have keys: driver_title, driver_query, source_titles.
559	    Returns a list of extraction payloads in the same order.
560	    """
561	    if not drivers or not llm_enabled:
562	        return [
563	            extract_indicator_candidates_for_driver_payload(
564	                driver_title=str(d.get("driver_title", "")),
565	                driver_query=str(d.get("driver_query", "")),
566	                source_titles=list(d.get("source_titles", [])),
567	                max_indicators=max_indicators,
568	                llm_enabled=llm_enabled,
569	                fallback_pack_name=fallback_pack_name,
570	            )
571	            for d in drivers
572	        ]
573	
574	    # Sequential per-driver extraction. The batch API adds 600s polling
575	    # overhead that exceeds the time to run 16 Sonnet calls sequentially.
576	    payloads = []
577	    for d in drivers:
578	        payloads.append(
579	            extract_indicator_candidates_for_driver_payload(
580	                driver_title=str(d.get("driver_title", "")),
581	                driver_query=str(d.get("driver_query", "")),
582	                source_titles=list(d.get("source_titles", [])),
583	                max_indicators=max_indicators,
584	                llm_enabled=llm_enabled,
585	                fallback_pack_name=fallback_pack_name,
586	            )
587	        )
588	    return payloads
589	
590	
591	# Backward-compatible alias for the old internal name
592	_normalize_indicator_rows = normalize_indicator_rows
593

53	
54	def _norm(text: str) -> str:
55	    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", str(text or "").lower()).strip()
56	
57	
58	def _strip_driver_prefix(label: str) -> str:
59	    return re.sub(r"^\s*driver\s*:\s*", "", str(label or "").strip(), flags=re.IGNORECASE)
60	
61	
62	def _driver_nodes_index(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> tuple[dict[str, NodeId], dict[str, NodeId], dict[str, NodeId]]:
63	    by_title: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
64	    by_query: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
65	    by_id: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
66	    for node in getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).values():
67	        if str(getattr(node, "type", "")).strip() != "driver":
68	            continue
69	        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
70	        if node_question_id(data) != question_id:
71	            continue
72	        node_id = str(getattr(node, "id", "")).strip()
73	        if node_id:
74	            by_id[node_id] = node_id
75	        label = str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
76	        for candidate in (
77	            label,
78	            _strip_driver_prefix(label),
79	            str(node_detail_value(data, "summary") or "").strip(),
80	            str(node_detail_value(data, "event") or "").strip(),
81	        ):
82	            key = _norm(candidate)
83	            if key and key not in by_title:
84	                by_title[key] = node_id
85	        search_query = _norm(str(node_detail_value(data, "search_query") or "").strip())
86	        if search_query and search_query not in by_query:
87	            by_query[search_query] = node_id
88	    return by_title, by_query, by_id
89	
90	
91	def _indicator_identity_key(*, driver_id: str, indicator_label: str) -> str:
92	    return f"{str(driver_id or '').strip()}\x1f{_norm(indicator_label)}"
93	
94	
95	def _legacy_indicator_identity_key(indicator_label: str) -> str:
96	    return f"legacy\x1f{_norm(indicator_label)}"
97	
98	
99	def _existing_indicator_nodes(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> dict[str, NodeId]:
100	    out: dict[str, NodeId] = {}
101	    for node in getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).values():
102	        if str(getattr(node, "type", "")).strip() != "indicator":
103	            continue
104	        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
105	        if node_question_id(data) != question_id:
106	            continue
107	        detail = dict(data.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("detail"), dict) else {}
108	        driver_id = str(detail.get("parent_driver_id") or detail.get("driver_id") or "").strip()
109	        label = str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
110	        key = _indicator_identity_key(driver_id=driver_id, indicator_label=label) if driver_id else _legacy_indicator_identity_key(label)
111	        if key and key not in out:
112	            out[key] = str(getattr(node, "id", "")).strip()
113	    return out
114	
115	
116	def _normalize_selected_sources(items: object) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
117	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
118	    seen: set[tuple[str, ...]] = set()
119	    for item in items or []:
120	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
121	            continue
122	        normalized = {str(key): value for key, value in item.items() if value not in (None, "", [], {})}
123	        if not normalized:
124	            continue
125	        key = (
126	            str(normalized.get("title") or "").strip(),
127	            str(normalized.get("entry_id") or "").strip(),
128	            str(normalized.get("pdf_url") or "").strip(),
129	            str(normalized.get("pdf_filename") or normalized.get("local_path") or "").strip(),
130	        )
131	        if key in seen:
132	            continue
133	        seen.add(key)
134	        out.append(normalized)
135	    return out
136	
137	
138	def _merged_sources(existing: object, incoming: object) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
139	    merged = _normalize_selected_sources(existing)
140	    existing_keys = {
141	        (
142	            str(item.get("title") or "").strip(),
143	            str(item.get("entry_id") or "").strip(),
144	            str(item.get("pdf_url") or "").strip(),
145	            str(item.get("pdf_filename") or item.get("local_path") or "").strip(),
146	        )
147	        for item in merged
148	    }
149	    for item in _normalize_selected_sources(incoming):
150	        key = (
151	            str(item.get("title") or "").strip(),
152	            str(item.get("entry_id") or "").strip(),
153	            str(item.get("pdf_url") or "").strip(),
154	            str(item.get("pdf_filename") or item.get("local_path") or "").strip(),
155	        )
156	        if key in existing_keys:
157	            continue
158	        existing_keys.add(key)
159	        merged.append(item)
160	    return merged
161	
162	
163	def _edge_key(edge_type: str, from_id: str, to_id: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
164	    return (edge_type, from_id, to_id)
165	
166	
167	def _source_key(value: object) -> str:
168	    return str(value or "").strip().lower()
169	
170	
171	def _source_keys_from_payload(source: Mapping[str, Any], *, label: str = "", detail: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> set[str]:
172	    detail_payload = dict(detail or {})
173	    keys: set[str] = set()
174	    candidates = [
175	        label,
176	        source.get("source_id"),
177	        source.get("evidence_node_id"),
178	        source.get("document_key"),
179	        source.get("title"),
180	        source.get("entry_id"),
181	        source.get("pdf_url"),
182	        source.get("source_url"),
183	        source.get("pdf_filename"),
184	        source.get("filename"),
185	        source.get("local_path"),
186	        source.get("source_path"),
187	        detail_payload.get("document_key"),
188	        detail_payload.get("source_id"),
189	        detail_payload.get("evidence_node_id"),
190	        detail_payload.get("document_link"),
191	        detail_payload.get("filename"),
192	        detail_payload.get("title"),
193	        detail_payload.get("source_url"),
194	        detail_payload.get("pdf_url"),
195	    ]
196	    try:
197	        candidates.append(canonical_document_key_from_source(source=source, detail=detail_payload, label=label))
198	    except Exception:
199	        pass
200	    for candidate in candidates:
201	        text = str(candidate or "").strip()
202	        if not text:
203	            continue
204	        keys.add(_source_key(text))
205	        normalized = _norm(text)
206	        if normalized:
207	            keys.add(normalized)
208	        basename = Path(text).name
209	        if basename and basename != text:
210	            keys.add(_source_key(basename))
211	            normalized_basename = _norm(basename)
212	            if normalized_basename:
213	                keys.add(normalized_basename)
214	    return keys
215	
216	
217	def _information_source_lookup(graph: Any, *, question_id: str) -> dict[str, str]:
218	    lookup: dict[str, str] = {}
219	    for node in getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).values():
220	        if str(getattr(node, "type", "") or "").strip().lower() not in {"evidence", "information"}:
221	            continue
222	        data = getattr(node, "data", {}) or {}
223	        if node_question_id(data) != question_id:
224	            continue
225	        node_id = str(getattr(node, "id", "") or "").strip()
226	        if not node_id:
227	            continue
228	        label = str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
229	        detail = dict(data.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("detail"), dict) else {}
230	        provenance = node_provenance(data)
231	        source_items = [item for item in (provenance.get("sources") or []) if isinstance(item, Mapping)] or [{}]
232	        node_keys = {node_id, _source_key(node_id), _norm(node_id)}
233	        try:
234	            node_keys.add(document_key_from_node(label=label, data=data))
235	        except Exception:
236	            pass
237	        for source in source_items:
238	            node_keys.update(_source_keys_from_payload(source, label=label, detail=detail))
239	        for key in node_keys:
240	            if key and key not in lookup:
241	                lookup[key] = node_id
242	    return lookup
243	
244	
245	def _resolved_information_ids_for_sources(
246	    *,
247	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
248	    information_lookup: dict[str, str],
249	) -> list[str]:
250	    resolved: list[str] = []
251	    seen: set[str] = set()
252	    for source in selected_sources:
253	        for key in _source_keys_from_payload(source, label=str(source.get("title") or "")):
254	            node_id = information_lookup.get(key)
255	            if not node_id or node_id in seen:
256	                continue
257	            seen.add(node_id)
258	            resolved.append(node_id)
259	            break
260	    return resolved
261	
262	
263	def _indicator_is_inferred(indicator_row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
264	    source_mode = str(indicator_row.get("source_mode") or "").strip()
265	    epistemic_status = str(indicator_row.get("epistemic_status") or "").strip()
266	    return source_mode in {"pack_fallback_indicator", "llm_best_effort_no_sources"} or epistemic_status == "hypothesis"
267	
268	
269	def _create_indicator_reference_edges(
270	    graph: Any,
271	    *,
272	    ind: Mapping[str, Any],
273	    indicator_id: str,
274	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
275	    information_lookup: dict[str, str],
276	    toi: datetime | None,
277	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig,
278	) -> tuple[int, int]:
279	    if _indicator_is_inferred(ind):
280	        return 0, 0
281	    information_ids = _resolved_information_ids_for_sources(selected_sources=selected_sources, information_lookup=information_lookup)
282	    created = 0
283	    skipped = 0
284	    for information_id in information_ids:
285	        if cfg.dedupe_edges and getattr(graph, "_edge_keys", None) is not None:
286	            if _edge_key(cfg.reference_edge_type, information_id, indicator_id) in graph._edge_keys:
287	                skipped += 1
288	                continue
289	        graph.new_edge(
290	            from_id=information_id,
291	            to_id=indicator_id,
292	            edge_type=cfg.reference_edge_type,
293	            weight=1.0,
294	            toi=toi,
295	            reference_type="source",
296	        )
297	        created += 1
298	    return created, skipped
299	
300	
301	def _strip_indicator_relation_detail(data: object) -> dict[str, Any]:
302	    payload = dict(data or {}) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
303	    payload.pop("rationale", None)
304	    payload.pop("confidence", None)
305	    detail = dict(payload.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(payload.get("detail"), dict) else {}
306	    detail.pop("rationale", None)
307	    detail.pop("confidence", None)
308	    if detail:
309	        payload["detail"] = detail
310	    else:
311	        payload.pop("detail", None)
312	    return payload
313	
314	
315	def _indicator_detail(
316	    indicator_row: Mapping[str, Any],
317	    *,
318	    parent_driver_id: str = "",
319	    parent_driver_label: str = "",
320	) -> dict[str, Any]:
321	    detail: dict[str, Any] = {}
322	    for key in ("source_mode", "epistemic_status", "human_review_rationale"):
323	        value = str(indicator_row.get(key) or "").strip()
324	        if value:
325	            detail[key] = value
326	    if str(parent_driver_id or "").strip():
327	        detail["indicator_scope"] = "driver"
328	        detail["parent_driver_id"] = str(parent_driver_id or "").strip()
329	    if str(parent_driver_label or "").strip():
330	        detail["parent_driver_label"] = str(parent_driver_label or "").strip()
331	    return detail
332

334	@dataclass
335	class _IndicatorApplyStats:
336	    """Mutable accumulator for indicator application counts."""
337	
338	    indicators_added: int = 0
339	    edges_added: int = 0
340	    driver_rows_processed: int = 0
341	    missing_driver_rows: int = 0
342	    fallback_indicator_count: int = 0
343	    llm_indicator_count: int = 0
344	    review_required_driver_rows: int = 0
345	    fallback_driver_rows: int = 0
346	    drivers_without_indicators: int = 0
347	    indicator_reference_edges_added: int = 0
348	    indicator_reference_edges_skipped: int = 0
349	
350	
351	def _collect_eligible_drivers(
352	    rows: list[Any],
353	    *,
354	    driver_lookup: dict[str, str],
355	    driver_query_lookup: dict[str, str],
356	    driver_id_lookup: dict[str, str],
357	    per_driver: list[dict[str, Any]],
358	    stats: _IndicatorApplyStats,
359	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
360	    """Match driver rows to graph nodes. Returns (eligible_drivers, eligible_meta)."""
361	    eligible_drivers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
362	    eligible_meta: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
363	    for row in rows:
364	        if not isinstance(row, dict):
365	            continue
366	        driver_title = str(row.get("driver_title") or "").strip()
367	        driver_search_query = str(row.get("driver_search_query") or "").strip()
368	        if not driver_title and not driver_search_query:
369	            continue
370	        row_driver_id = str(row.get("driver_id") or row.get("graph_driver_id") or "").strip()
371	        driver_id = driver_id_lookup.get(row_driver_id, "") if row_driver_id else ""
372	        if not driver_id:
373	            driver_id = driver_lookup.get(_norm(driver_title), "")
374	        if not driver_id:
375	            query_key = _norm(driver_search_query)
376	            if query_key:
377	                driver_id = driver_query_lookup.get(query_key, "")
378	        if not driver_id:
379	            stats.missing_driver_rows += 1
380	            per_driver.append(
381	                {
382	                    "driver_title": driver_title,
383	                    "status": "missing_driver_node",
384	                    "indicator_count": 0,
385	                    "edges_added": 0,
386	                    "source_row_status": str(row.get("status") or "").strip(),
387	                }
388	            )
389	            continue
390	        selected_sources = _normalize_selected_sources(row.get("items") or row.get("selected_sources") or [])
391	        source_titles = [str(s.get("title") or "").strip() for s in selected_sources if str(s.get("title") or "").strip()]
392	        eligible_drivers.append({"driver_title": driver_title, "driver_query": driver_search_query, "source_titles": source_titles})
393	        eligible_meta.append({"row": row, "driver_id": driver_id, "driver_title": driver_title, "selected_sources": selected_sources})
394	    return eligible_drivers, eligible_meta
395	
396	
397	def _upsert_indicator_node(
398	    graph: Any,
399	    *,
400	    ind: Mapping[str, Any],
401	    indicator_lookup: dict[str, str],
402	    question_id: str,
403	    question_source: str,
404	    driver_id: str,
405	    driver_title: str,
406	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
407	    toi: datetime | None,
408	) -> tuple[str, bool]:
409	    """Create or update an indicator node. Returns (indicator_id, was_created)."""
410	    name = str(ind.get("name") or "").strip()
411	    key = _indicator_identity_key(driver_id=driver_id, indicator_label=name)
412	    indicator_id = indicator_lookup.get(key, "")
413	    if not indicator_id:
414	        legacy_key = _legacy_indicator_identity_key(name)
415	        indicator_id = indicator_lookup.pop(legacy_key, "")
416	        if indicator_id:
417	            indicator_lookup[key] = indicator_id
418	    if not indicator_id:
419	        origin_stage = str(ind.get("origin_stage") or "indicator_extraction").strip() or "indicator_extraction"
420	        indicator_id = graph.new_node(
421	            "indicator",
422	            label=name,
423	            toi=toi,
424	            **build_node_data(
425	                question_id=question_id,
426	                question_source=question_source,
427	                origin=build_origin(producer="indicator_literature", stage=origin_stage, question_source=question_source),
428	                provenance=build_provenance(source_kind="driver_indicator_literature", sources=selected_sources),
429	                detail=_indicator_detail(ind, parent_driver_id=driver_id, parent_driver_label=driver_title),
430	            ),
431	        )
432	        indicator_lookup[key] = indicator_id
433	        return indicator_id, True
434	
435	    node = getattr(graph, "nodes", {}).get(indicator_id)
436	    if node is not None:
437	        existing_data = _strip_indicator_relation_detail(getattr(node, "data", {}) or {})
438	        merged_sources = _merged_sources(node_provenance(existing_data).get("sources"), selected_sources)
439	        merged_detail = dict(existing_data.get("detail") or {}) if isinstance(existing_data.get("detail"), dict) else {}
440	        merged_detail.update(_indicator_detail(ind, parent_driver_id=driver_id, parent_driver_label=driver_title))
441	        origin_stage = str(ind.get("origin_stage") or "indicator_extraction").strip() or "indicator_extraction"
442	        node.data = build_node_data(
443	            question_id=question_id,
444	            question_source=question_source,
445	            origin=build_origin(producer="indicator_literature", stage=origin_stage, question_source=question_source),
446	            provenance=build_provenance(source_kind="driver_indicator_literature", sources=merged_sources),
447	            detail=merged_detail or None,
448	            extra=existing_data,
449	        )
450	    return indicator_id, False
451	
452	
453	def _create_indicator_edge(
454	    graph: Any,
455	    *,
456	    ind: Mapping[str, Any],
457	    indicator_id: str,
458	    driver_id: str,
459	    selected_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],
460	    toi: datetime | None,
461	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig,
462	) -> None:
463	    """Create a CONDITIONAL edge from indicator to driver."""
464	    direction = str(ind.get("direction") or "supports").strip().lower()
465	    if direction not in {"supports", "contradicts"}:
466	        direction = "supports"
467	    rj = normalize_relationship_judgment(ind.get("relationship_judgment"), fallback=relationship_judgment_from_direction(direction))
468	    odds = relationship_judgment_odds_multiplier(rj, fallback=relationship_judgment_from_direction(direction))
469	    extraction_confidence = ind.get("confidence")
470	    edge_data: dict[str, Any] = {
471	        "relation": "indicator_to_driver",
472	        "direction": direction,
473	        "relationship_judgment": rj,
474	        "odds_multiplier": odds,
475	        "relationship_description": str(ind.get("relationship_description") or "").strip() or str(ind.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
476	        "rationale": str(ind.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
477	        "confidence": extraction_confidence,
478	        "extraction_confidence": extraction_confidence,
479	        "sources": selected_sources,
480	    }
481	    edge_data["edge_quality"] = grade_conditional_edge_quality(graph=graph, from_id=indicator_id, to_id=driver_id, edge_data=edge_data)
482	    graph.new_edge(
483	        from_id=indicator_id,
484	        to_id=driver_id,
485	        edge_type=cfg.conditional_edge_type,
486	        weight=float(cfg.conditional_edge_weight) * odds,
487	        toi=toi,
488	        **edge_data,
489	    )
490	
491	
492	def _build_driver_summary(
493	    *,
494	    driver_title: str,
495	    indicators: list[dict[str, Any]],
496	    created_for_driver: int,
497	    edges_for_driver: int,
498	    reference_edges_added: int,
499	    extraction_payload: dict[str, Any],
500	    source_row_status: str,
501	) -> dict[str, Any]:
502	    """Build the per-driver summary dict for the output payload."""
503	    return {
504	        "driver_title": driver_title,
505	        "status": "ok" if indicators else "review_required_no_fallback",
506	        "indicator_count": len([x for x in indicators if isinstance(x, dict)]),
507	        "indicators_created": created_for_driver,
508	        "edges_added": edges_for_driver,
509	        "reference_edges_added": reference_edges_added,
510	        "fallback_used": bool(extraction_payload.get("fallback_used")),
511	        "review_required": bool(extraction_payload.get("review_required")),
512	        "message": str(extraction_payload.get("message") or "").strip(),
513	        "pack_name": str(extraction_payload.get("pack_name") or "").strip(),
514	        "fallback_policy": str(extraction_payload.get("fallback_policy") or "").strip(),
515	        "fallback_support": str(extraction_payload.get("fallback_support") or "").strip(),
516	        "stage_outcome": dict(extraction_payload.get("stage_outcome") or {}),
517	        "source_row_status": source_row_status,
518	        "indicators": [
519	            {
520	                "name": str(i.get("name") or "").strip(),
521	                "direction": str(i.get("direction") or "supports").strip(),
522	                "relationship_judgment": str(i.get("relationship_judgment") or "").strip(),
523	                "rationale": str(i.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
524	                "confidence": i.get("confidence"),
525	                "source_mode": str(i.get("source_mode") or "").strip(),
526	                "epistemic_status": str(i.get("epistemic_status") or "").strip(),
527	            }
528	            for i in indicators
529	            if isinstance(i, dict) and str(i.get("name") or "").strip()
530	        ],
531	    }
532	
533	
534	def _apply_single_driver_extraction(
535	    graph: Any,
536	    *,
537	    meta: dict[str, Any],
538	    extraction_payload: dict[str, Any],
539	    indicator_lookup: dict[str, str],
540	    question_id: str,
541	    question_source: str,
542	    toi: datetime | None,
543	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig,
544	    stats: _IndicatorApplyStats,
545	    information_lookup: dict[str, str],
546	) -> dict[str, Any]:
547	    driver_id, driver_title, selected_sources = meta["driver_id"], meta["driver_title"], meta["selected_sources"]
548	    indicators = [x for x in (extraction_payload.get("indicators") or []) if isinstance(x, dict)]
549	    if extraction_payload.get("review_required"):
550	        stats.review_required_driver_rows += 1
551	    if extraction_payload.get("fallback_used"):
552	        stats.fallback_driver_rows += 1
553	    created_for_driver = 0
554	    edges_for_driver = 0
555	    reference_edges_for_driver = 0
556	    for ind in indicators:
557	        if not isinstance(ind, dict):
558	            continue
559	        name = str(ind.get("name") or "").strip()
560	        if not name or not _norm(name):
561	            continue
562	        if str(ind.get("source_mode") or "").strip() == "pack_fallback_indicator":
563	            stats.fallback_indicator_count += 1
564	        else:
565	            stats.llm_indicator_count += 1
566	        indicator_id, was_created = _upsert_indicator_node(
567	            graph,
568	            ind=ind,
569	            indicator_lookup=indicator_lookup,
570	            question_id=question_id,
571	            question_source=question_source,
572	            driver_id=driver_id,
573	            driver_title=driver_title,
574	            selected_sources=selected_sources,
575	            toi=toi,
576	        )
577	        if was_created:
578	            stats.indicators_added += 1
579	            created_for_driver += 1
580	        reference_edges_added, reference_edges_skipped = _create_indicator_reference_edges(
581	            graph,
582	            ind=ind,
583	            indicator_id=indicator_id,
584	            selected_sources=selected_sources,
585	            information_lookup=information_lookup,
586	            toi=toi,
587	            cfg=cfg,
588	        )
589	        stats.indicator_reference_edges_added += reference_edges_added
590	        stats.indicator_reference_edges_skipped += reference_edges_skipped
591	        reference_edges_for_driver += reference_edges_added
592	        if cfg.dedupe_edges and getattr(graph, "_edge_keys", None) is not None:
593	            if _edge_key(cfg.conditional_edge_type, indicator_id, driver_id) in graph._edge_keys:
594	                continue
595	        _create_indicator_edge(
596	            graph, ind=ind, indicator_id=indicator_id, driver_id=driver_id, selected_sources=selected_sources, toi=toi, cfg=cfg
597	        )
598	        stats.edges_added += 1
599	        edges_for_driver += 1
600	
601	    stats.driver_rows_processed += 1
602	    if not indicators:
603	        stats.drivers_without_indicators += 1
604	    return _build_driver_summary(
605	        driver_title=driver_title,
606	        indicators=indicators,
607	        created_for_driver=created_for_driver,
608	        edges_for_driver=edges_for_driver,
609	        extraction_payload=extraction_payload,
610	        source_row_status=str(meta["row"].get("status") or "").strip(),
611	        reference_edges_added=reference_edges_for_driver,
612	    )
613	
614	
615	def _compute_indicator_stage_outcome(
616	    *,
617	    per_driver_stage_statuses: list[str],
618	    stats: _IndicatorApplyStats,
619	) -> dict[str, Any]:
620	    s = stats
621	    ce: dict[str, Any] = {
622	        "driver_rows_processed": s.driver_rows_processed,
623	        "llm_indicator_candidate_count": s.llm_indicator_count,
624	        "fallback_indicator_candidate_count": s.fallback_indicator_count,
625	    }
626	    if not per_driver_stage_statuses:
627	        return build_stage_outcome(
628	            status="failed",
629	            reason_code="indicator_no_driver_rows",
630	            message="Indicator extraction did not process any driver rows.",
631	            fallback_used=False,
632	            quality_flags=["no_driver_rows"],
633	            extra={
634	                "driver_rows_processed": s.driver_rows_processed,
635	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
636	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
637	            },
638	        )
639	    if (
640	        s.review_required_driver_rows > 0
641	        and s.review_required_driver_rows == s.driver_rows_processed
642	        and s.drivers_without_indicators == s.driver_rows_processed
643	        and s.missing_driver_rows == 0
644	    ):
645	        return build_stage_outcome(
646	            status="partial",
647	            reason_code="indicator_review_required_no_candidates",
648	            message="Indicator extraction could not ground indicators for the processed drivers.",
649	            fallback_used=False,
650	            quality_flags=["review_required_driver_rows", "no_indicator_candidates"],
651	            extra={
652	                **ce,
653	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
654	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
655	            },
656	        )
657	    if s.drivers_without_indicators > 0:
658	        return build_stage_outcome(
659	            status="failed",
660	            reason_code="indicator_missing_for_driver_rows",
661	            message="Indicator extraction did not produce at least one usable indicator for every driver row.",
662	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
663	            quality_flags=["missing_indicator_driver_rows"] + (["pack_fallback_used"] if s.fallback_driver_rows > 0 else []),
664	            extra={
665	                **ce,
666	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
667	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
668	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
669	            },
670	        )
671	    if s.missing_driver_rows > 0:
672	        return build_stage_outcome(
673	            status="partial",
674	            reason_code="indicator_missing_driver_nodes",
675	            message="Indicator extraction completed for matched driver rows, but some literature rows could not be matched to driver nodes.",
676	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
677	            quality_flags=["missing_driver_nodes"] + (["pack_fallback_used"] if s.fallback_driver_rows > 0 else []),
678	            extra={
679	                **ce,
680	                "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
681	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
682	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
683	            },
684	        )
685	    if all(st == "ok" for st in per_driver_stage_statuses):
686	        return build_stage_outcome(
687	            status="ok",
688	            reason_code="indicator_llm_generated",
689	            message="Indicator extraction completed without degraded paths.",
690	            fallback_used=False,
691	            quality_flags=[],
692	            extra={**ce, "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators},
693	        )
694	    if any(st in ("ok", "partial") for st in per_driver_stage_statuses):
695	        return build_stage_outcome(
696	            status="partial",
697	            reason_code="indicator_mixed_quality",
698	            message="Indicator extraction completed with fallback or missing-indicator rows.",
699	            fallback_used=s.fallback_driver_rows > 0,
700	            quality_flags=["mixed_driver_rows"] + (["pack_fallback_used"] if s.fallback_driver_rows > 0 else []),
701	            extra={
702	                **ce,
703	                "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
704	                "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
705	            },
706	        )
707	    return build_stage_outcome(
708	        status="failed",
709	        reason_code="indicator_no_candidates",
710	        message="Indicator extraction produced no usable indicators for the processed drivers.",
711	        fallback_used=False,
712	        quality_flags=["no_indicator_candidates"],
713	        extra={
714	            **ce,
715	            "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
716	            "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
717	        },
718	    )
719	
720	
721	def apply_indicators_from_driver_literature(
722	    graph: Any,
723	    *,
724	    question_id: str,
725	    indicator_literature_payload: Mapping[str, Any],
726	    toi: datetime | None,
727	    cfg: IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig = IndicatorGraphApplicationConfig(),
728	) -> dict[str, Any]:
729	    """Create indicator nodes and indicator->driver CONDITIONAL edges."""
730	    rows_payload = indicator_literature_payload.get("drivers")
731	    if not isinstance(rows_payload, list):
732	        rows_payload = indicator_literature_payload.get("results")
733	    rows = rows_payload if isinstance(rows_payload, list) else []
734	
735	    driver_lookup, driver_query_lookup, driver_id_lookup = _driver_nodes_index(graph, question_id=question_id)
736	    indicator_lookup = _existing_indicator_nodes(graph, question_id=question_id)
737	    question_source = (
738	        metadata_text(
739	            getattr(graph, "question_metadata", {}) or {},
740	            "question_source",
741	            "unknown",
742	            aliases=("question_source", "source_tag"),
743	        )
744	        or "unknown"
745	    )
746	
747	    stats = _IndicatorApplyStats()
748	    per_driver: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
749	    per_driver_stage_statuses: list[str] = []
750	
751	    eligible_drivers, eligible_meta = _collect_eligible_drivers(
752	        rows,
753	        driver_lookup=driver_lookup,
754	        driver_query_lookup=driver_query_lookup,
755	        driver_id_lookup=driver_id_lookup,
756	        per_driver=per_driver,
757	        stats=stats,
758	    )
759	    information_lookup = _information_source_lookup(graph, question_id=question_id)
760	
761	    extraction_payloads = batch_extract_indicator_candidates(
762	        eligible_drivers,
763	        max_indicators=max(1, int(cfg.max_indicators_per_driver)),
764	        llm_enabled=bool(cfg.llm_enabled),
765	        fallback_pack_name=str(cfg.fallback_pack_name or "generic_arxiv"),
766	    )
767	
768	    for meta, extraction_payload in zip(eligible_meta, extraction_payloads):
769	        driver_summary = _apply_single_driver_extraction(
770	            graph,
771	            meta=meta,
772	            extraction_payload=extraction_payload,
773	            indicator_lookup=indicator_lookup,
774	            question_id=question_id,
775	            question_source=question_source,
776	            toi=toi,
777	            cfg=cfg,
778	            stats=stats,
779	            information_lookup=information_lookup,
780	        )
781	        driver_stage_outcome = dict(extraction_payload.get("stage_outcome") or {})
782	        per_driver_stage_statuses.append(stage_status(driver_stage_outcome, "failed"))
783	        per_driver.append(driver_summary)
784	
785	    stage_outcome = _compute_indicator_stage_outcome(per_driver_stage_statuses=per_driver_stage_statuses, stats=stats)
786	    s = stats
787	    return {
788	        "question_id": question_id,
789	        "counts": {
790	            "driver_rows_processed": s.driver_rows_processed,
791	            "missing_driver_rows": s.missing_driver_rows,
792	            "indicators_added": s.indicators_added,
793	            "indicator_edges_added": s.edges_added,
794	            "fallback_indicators_added": s.fallback_indicator_count,
795	            "fallback_indicator_candidate_count": s.fallback_indicator_count,
796	            "llm_indicators_added": s.llm_indicator_count,
797	            "llm_indicator_candidate_count": s.llm_indicator_count,
798	            "fallback_driver_rows": s.fallback_driver_rows,
799	            "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
800	            "drivers_without_indicators": s.drivers_without_indicators,
801	            "indicator_reference_edges_added": s.indicator_reference_edges_added,
802	            "indicator_reference_edges_skipped": s.indicator_reference_edges_skipped,
803	        },
804	        "indicator_extraction": {
805	            "pack_name": str(cfg.fallback_pack_name or "generic_arxiv"),
806	            "fallback_driver_rows": s.fallback_driver_rows,
807	            "review_required_driver_rows": s.review_required_driver_rows,
808	        },
809	        "stage_outcome": stage_outcome,
810	        "drivers": per_driver,
811	    }
812

41	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
42	# Centralised decisions for graph mutation
43	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
44	# These are â€œbehavioural knobsâ€ that the step function/orchestrator should own.
45	# We expose them here so:
46	#   - edge types/weights are not hard-coded in multiple places
47	#   - warning vs hard-fail behaviour is configurable centrally
48	#   - logging verbosity is controlled in one place
49	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
50	
51	
52	@dataclass(frozen=True)
53	class DriverGraphApplicationConfig:
54	    """
55	    Configuration for applying reduced factors to the BayesianGraph.
56	
57	    Decisions captured here:
58	    - reference_edge_type: edge_type string used for evidenceâ†’driver links
59	    - reference_edge_weight: weight assigned to REFERENCE edges (not an odds multiplier;
60	      it's just "there is a citation" â€” keep at 1 unless you change semantics)
61	    - warn_on_missing_evidence_ref: if True, log warnings when a factor references an
62	      evidence ref that cannot be resolved; otherwise stay silent.
63	    - conditional_edge_type: edge_type used for driver->target links
64	    - conditional_edge_weight: default weight for CONDITIONAL edges
65	    - add_driver_to_target_edges: if True, add driver->target CONDITIONAL edges
66	    - dedupe_edges: if True, skip adding edges whose key is already present in graph._edge_keys
67	      (assumes your graph maintains this set consistently)
68	    """
69	
70	    reference_edge_type: str = "REFERENCE"
71	    reference_edge_weight: float = 1.0
72	    conditional_edge_type: str = "CONDITIONAL"
73	    conditional_edge_weight: float = 1.0
74	    add_driver_to_target_edges: bool = True
75	    add_low_confidence_edges_for_unresolved: bool = True
76	    warn_on_missing_evidence_ref: bool = True
77	    dedupe_edges: bool = True
78	    grade_edge_quality: bool = False
79
